which gives us the kinetic dissipation. Next, by the second Ampère’s equation and the third Faraday’s equation in (
3.2
), we compute that

(3.6)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle(\partial^{m}_{x}E\cdot v)\sqrt{M}\mathsf{q}_{1},\partial^{m}_{x}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}=-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1}v\sqrt{M}\mathrm{d}v\right)\cdot\partial^{m}_{x}E\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left(\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}E-\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}B\right)\cdot\partial^{m}_{x}E\mathrm{d}x=\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}-\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}(\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}B)\cdot\partial^{m}_{x}E\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}-\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}(\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}E)\cdot\partial^{m}_{x}B\mathrm{d}x=\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\cdot\partial^{m}_{x}B\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\right)\,,

where we utilize the relation
\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}(\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}B)\cdot\partial^{m}_{x}E\mathrm{d}x=\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}(\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}E)\cdot\partial^{m}_{x}B\mathrm{d}x
.

We now deal with the term
\tfrac{1}{2}\left\langle\mathsf{q}\partial^{m}_{x}[(E\cdot v)G],\partial^{m}_{x}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}
for all
|m|\leq s
. We make use of the decomposition
G=\mathbb{P}G+\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
and then obtain

(3.7)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\left\langle\mathsf{q}\partial^{m}_{x}[(E\cdot v)G],\partial^{m}_{x}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle(\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\cdot v)\left(\mathsf{q}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G+\mathsf{q}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right),\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G+\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{A}_{1}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle(\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\cdot v)\mathsf{q}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle+\underset{\textbf{A}_{2}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle(\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\cdot v)\mathsf{q}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle+\underset{\textbf{A}_{3}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle(\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\cdot v)\mathsf{q}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle+\underset{\textbf{A}_{4}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle(\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\cdot v)\mathsf{q}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}\,.

For the term
\textbf{A}_{1}
, we derive from the definition of (
1.17
), the Hölder inequality, the Sobolev embeddings
H^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{\infty}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
,
H^{1}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{4}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
that for all
|m|\leq s\,(s\geq 2)

(3.8)

\displaystyle\textbf{A}_{1}\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}<m}\left\langle|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E|\,|v\mathsf{q}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G|\,,|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G|\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+C\left\langle|\partial^{m}_{x}E|\,|v\mathsf{q}\mathbb{P}G|+|E|\,|v\mathsf{q}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G|\,,|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G|\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}<m}\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{4}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+C\left(\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right)\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\,.

For the terms
\textbf{A}_{2}
and
\textbf{A}_{3}
, we similarly have

(3.9)

\textbf{A}_{2}+\textbf{A}_{3}\leq C\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\,.

For the term
\textbf{A}_{4}
,

(3.10)

\displaystyle\textbf{A}_{4}\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}<m}\left\langle|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E|\,|\nu\mathsf{q}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G|\,,|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G|\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+C\left\langle|\partial^{m}_{x}E|\,|\nu\mathsf{q}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G|+|E|\,|\nu(v)\mathsf{q}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G|\,,|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G|\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}<m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{4}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\left(\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+\|E\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\right)\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\,,

holds for all
|m|\leq s\,(s\geq 2)
, which is derived from the fact
|v|\leq\nu(v)
(implied by the part (1) of Lemma
2.1
), the Hölder inequality, the relation (
2.27
), i.e.,
\big\|\nabla_{x}\|g\|_{L^{2}_{v}(w)}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq\|\nabla_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(w)}
and the Sobolev embeddings
H^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{\infty}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
,
H^{1}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{4}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
. In summary, we have

(3.11)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathsf{q}\partial^{m}_{x}[(E\cdot v)G],\partial^{m}_{x}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\|\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\right)\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\,.

We now estimate the term
\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\Gamma(G,H)\,,\partial^{m}_{x}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}
by employing Lemma
2.4
. More precisely,

(3.12)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\Gamma(G,G)\,,\partial^{m}_{x}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\Gamma(G,G)\,,\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\Gamma}}{\varepsilon}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\Gamma}}{\varepsilon}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\left(\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\right)\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\Gamma}}{\varepsilon}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\left(\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\right)\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\,.

It remains to estimate the term
-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum\limits_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\mathsf{q}\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}G,\partial^{m}_{x}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}
carefully. By using the relation
G=\mathbb{P}G+\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
, it can easily be decomposed as four parts:

(3.13)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\mathsf{q}\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}G,\partial^{m}_{x}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{B}_{1}}{\underbrace{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\mathsf{q}\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{B}_{2}}{\underbrace{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\mathsf{q}\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{B}_{3}}{\underbrace{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\mathsf{q}\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{B}_{4}}{\underbrace{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\mathsf{q}\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}\,.

The
key point
is to deal with the singularity
\frac{1}{\varepsilon}
occurring in the terms
\textbf{B}_{1}
,
\textbf{B}_{2}
,
\textbf{B}_{3}
and
\textbf{B}_{4}
. Thanks to the kinetic dissipation term
\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}
derived from the non-negativity of
\mathscr{L}
in Lemma
2.3
, the singular term
\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
in
\textbf{B}_{2}
,
\textbf{B}_{3}
and
\textbf{B}_{4}
will be absorbed after some subtle calculations. The singular part in
\textbf{B}_{1}
is actuary the term

\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum\limits_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\mathsf{q}\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}(v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\,.

Recalling the definition of
\mathbb{P}G
in (
1.15
), we have

(3.14)

\displaystyle\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}G=(u+v\theta)\mathsf{q}_{2}\sqrt{M}-\tfrac{1}{2}v\mathbb{P}G\,,

where
\mathsf{q}_{2}=[1,1]\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
,
\theta=\frac{1}{2}\left\langle G,\frac{2}{3}\phi_{6}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}
and the vector field
u=[u_{1},u_{2},u_{3}]\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
with the components
u_{i}=\frac{1}{2}\left\langle G,\phi_{2+i}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}
(
i=1,2,3
). Then we have

(3.15)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum\limits_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\mathsf{q}\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}(v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum\limits_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle v\times\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}B\cdot\left[(\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}u+v\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\theta)\mathsf{q}\mathsf{q}_{2}\sqrt{M}-\tfrac{1}{2}v\mathsf{q}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\right],\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum\limits_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle(v\times\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}B)\cdot\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}u,\mathsf{q}_{1}\sqrt{M}\cdot\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum\limits_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle(v\times\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}B)\cdot v,\left(\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\theta\mathsf{q}_{1}\sqrt{M}-\frac{1}{2}\mathsf{q}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\right)\cdot\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum\limits_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle(v\times\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}B)\cdot\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}u,\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle G,\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}M\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum\limits_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle(\left\langle v,M\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\times\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}B)\cdot\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}u,\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle G,\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\,,

where we make use of the cancellations
(v\times\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}B)\cdot v=0
,
\mathsf{q}_{1}\sqrt{M}\cdot\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G=\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle G,\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}M
and
\left\langle v,M\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}=0
. We thereby know that the term
\textbf{B}_{1}
does not involve the singularity.

Based on the above statements, we now estimate the terms
\textbf{B}_{1}
,
\textbf{B}_{2}
,
\textbf{B}_{3}
and
\textbf{B}_{4}
one by one. For the term
\textbf{B}_{1}
, we have

(3.16)

\displaystyle\textbf{B}_{1}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}<m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\mathsf{q}\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}-\left\langle\mathsf{q}\partial^{m}_{x}E\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}<m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{4}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+C\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

for all
|m|\leq s
(
s\geq 2
), where we make use of the Hölder inequality, the Sobolev embeddings
H^{1}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{4}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
and
H^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{\infty}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
, the definition (
1.17
) of
\mathbb{P}G
and the cancellation (
3.15
). For the term
\textbf{B}_{2}
, we derive from the similar arguments in estimating the term
\textbf{B}_{1}
that

(3.17)

\displaystyle\textbf{B}_{2}=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\mathsf{q}\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\partial^{m}_{x}\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}G,\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}<m}\left\langle\varepsilon|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E|+|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}B|,\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\varepsilon|\partial^{m}_{x}E|+|\partial^{m}_{x}B|,\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}<m}\left(\varepsilon\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\|_{L^{4}_{x}}+\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}B\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\right)\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{4}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\varepsilon}\left(\varepsilon\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\right)\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{\varepsilon}\left(\varepsilon\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\right)\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\,.

The term
\textbf{B}_{3}
can be similarly estimated as

(3.18)

\displaystyle\textbf{B}_{3}\leq\frac{C}{\varepsilon}\left(\varepsilon\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\right)\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\,.

We next control the term
\textbf{B}_{4}
. By the Hölder inequality, the relation
\max\{1,|v|\}\leq C\nu(v)
derived from the part (1) of Lemma
2.1
and the Sobolev embedding theories, we have

(3.19)

\displaystyle\textbf{B}_{4}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m^{\prime}|=1}\left\langle\varepsilon|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E|+\nu(v)|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}B|\,,|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G|\,|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G|\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{2\leq|m^{\prime}|<|m|}\left\langle\varepsilon|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E|+\nu(v)|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}B|\,,|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G|\,|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G|\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{C}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\varepsilon|\partial^{m}_{x}E|+\nu(v)|\partial^{m}_{x}B|\,,|\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G|\,|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G|\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m^{\prime}|=1}\left(\varepsilon\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}B\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right)\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{2\leq|m^{\prime}|<|m|}\left(\varepsilon\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\|_{L^{4}_{x}}+\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}B\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\right)\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{4}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\left(\varepsilon\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\right)\|\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\left(\varepsilon\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\right)\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\sum_{|m|\leq s-1}\|\nabla_{v}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

for all
|m|\leq s
and
s\geq 3
. We summarize the all estimates on terms
\textbf{B}_{1}
,
\textbf{B}_{2}
,
\textbf{B}_{3}
and
\textbf{B}_{4}
above and then we obtain

(3.20)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum\limits_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\mathsf{q}\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}G,\partial^{m}_{x}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\left(\varepsilon\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\right)\left(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\right)

\displaystyle\times\sum_{|m|\leq s-1}\|\nabla_{v}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}+C\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

for all
|m|\leq s
. Here we require the integer
s\geq 3
. Plugging the relations (
3.5
), (
3.6
), (
3.11
), (
3.12
) and (
3.20
) into the equality (
3.4
) reduces to

(3.21)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\|\partial^{m}_{x}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\right)+\tfrac{\lambda}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\left(\|\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\Big(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+\sum_{|m|\leq s-1}\|\nabla_{v}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\Big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{C}{\varepsilon}\left(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\right)\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\times\Big(\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+\sum_{|m|\leq s-1}\|\nabla_{v}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\Big)

for all multi-indexes
m\in\mathbb{N}^{3}
with
|m|\leq s
and
s\geq 3
. Summing up for all
|m|\leq s
in the above inequality implies the bound (
3.3
). Then the proof of Proposition
3.2
is finished.
∎

3.2.
Micro-macro decomposition on the equation of
G

In this subsection, we will find a dissipation of the fluid part
\mathbb{P}G
by using the so-called micro-macro decomposition method for the VMB version, which is inspired by that for the Boltzmann version. It is well-known that the micro-macro decomposition method for the Boltzmann version is actually depended on the so-called
thirteen moments
, see
[
32
]
for instance.

However, in order to obtain the dissipative term of the fluid part of the perturbed two species VMB system, we will introduce the following linear independent basis in
L^{2}_{v}

(3.22)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{B}=\Big\{[1,0]\sqrt{M},

\displaystyle[0,1]\sqrt{M},\ [v_{i},0]\sqrt{M},\ [0,v_{i}]\sqrt{M},\ [v_{i}^{2},v_{i}^{2}]\sqrt{M},

\displaystyle[v_{i}|v|^{2},v_{i}|v|^{2}]\sqrt{M},\ [v_{j}v_{k},v_{j}v_{k}]\sqrt{M};\ 1\leq i\leq 3,1\leq j<k\leq 3\Big\}\,,

which consists of seventeen linear independent moments. We call the basis
\mathfrak{B}
the
seventeen moments
. This can be seen in
[
31
]
, for instance. For notational simplicity, we denote by

(3.23)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{B}=\Big\{\beta^{\pm}(v),\ \beta_{i}^{\pm}(v),\ \beta_{i}(v),\ \widetilde{\beta}_{i}(v),\ \beta_{jk}(v);\ 1\leq i\leq 3,1\leq j<k\leq 3\Big\}\,,

where

(3.24)

\displaystyle\beta^{+}(v)=

\displaystyle[1,0]\sqrt{M(v)}\,,\qquad\ \ \beta^{-}(v)=[0,1]\sqrt{M(v)}\,,

\displaystyle\beta_{i}^{+}(v)=

\displaystyle[v_{i},0]\sqrt{M(v)}\,,\qquad\ \beta_{i}^{+}(v)=[0,v_{i}]\sqrt{M(v)}\,,

\displaystyle\beta_{i}(v)=

\displaystyle[v_{i}^{2},v_{i}^{2}]\sqrt{M(v)}\,,\qquad\widetilde{\beta}_{i}(v)=[v_{i}|v|^{2},v_{i}|v|^{2}]\sqrt{M(v)}\,,

\displaystyle\beta_{jk}(v)=

\displaystyle[v_{j}v_{k},v_{j}v_{k}]\sqrt{M(v)}\,.

One can easily justify that
\mathfrak{B}
is linearly independent in
L^{2}_{v}
. Indeed, if

(3.25)

\displaystyle\sum_{\pm}k_{\pm}\beta^{\pm}(v)+\sum_{\pm}\sum_{i=1}^{3}k_{i\pm}\beta_{i}^{\pm}(v)+\sum_{i=1}^{3}k_{i}\beta_{i}(v)+\sum_{i=1}^{3}\widetilde{k}_{i}\widetilde{\beta}_{i}(v)+\sum_{1\leq j<k\leq 3}k_{jk}\beta_{jk}(v)=0\,,

we take
L^{2}_{v}
-inner product in (
3.25
) by multiplying each element in the set
\mathfrak{B}
, and then we obtain

(3.26)

\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}k_{\pm}+\sum\limits_{i=1}^{3}k_{i}=0\,,\\
k_{i\pm}+5k_{i}=0\ (1\leq i\leq 3)\,,\\
k_{+}+k_{-}+6k_{i}=0\ (1\leq i\leq 3)\,,\\
k_{i+}+k_{i-}+2\widetilde{k}_{i}=0\ (1\leq i\leq 3)\,,\\
k_{jk}=0\ (1\leq j<k\leq 3)\,,\end{array}\right.

where we make use of the relations
\left\langle 1,M\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}=1
,
\left\langle|v|^{2},M\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}=3
and
\left\langle|v|^{4},M\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}=15
. Straightforward calculations imply that the linear system (
3.26
) admits only zero solution, namely,
k_{\pm}=k_{i\pm}=k_{i}=\widetilde{k}_{i}=k_{jk}=0
for
1\leq i\leq 3
and
1\leq j<k\leq 3
. Consequently, we know that
\mathfrak{B}
is linearly independent.

We now assume that
\{e_{j}\}_{1\leq j\leq 17}
is a orthonormal basis of the linear space
\textrm{Span}\{\mathfrak{B}\}
with dimensions 17. Then each
e_{j}
is a certain linear combination of
\mathfrak{B}
and
\textrm{Span}\{\mathfrak{B}\}=\textrm{Span}\{e_{j};1\leq j\leq 17\}\subset L^{2}_{v}
. We thereby define a projection
\mathcal{P}_{\mathfrak{B}}:L^{2}_{v}\rightarrow\textrm{Span}\{\mathfrak{B}\}\subset L^{2}_{v}
by

(3.27)

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{\mathfrak{B}}f=\sum_{j=1}^{17}\left\langle f,e_{j}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}e_{j}

for any
f\in L^{2}_{v}
. Since each
e_{j}
can be represented as a certain linear combination of
\mathfrak{B}
, the projection
\mathcal{P}_{\mathfrak{B}}
admits an equivalent form

(3.28)

\mathcal{P}_{\mathfrak{B}}f=\sum_{\pm}f^{\pm}\beta^{\pm}(v)+\sum_{\pm}\sum_{i=1}^{3}f_{i}^{\pm}\beta_{i}^{\pm}(v)+\sum_{i=1}^{3}f_{i}\beta_{i}(v)+\sum_{i=1}^{3}\widetilde{f}_{i}\widetilde{\beta}_{i}(v)+\sum_{1\leq i<j\leq 3}f_{ij}\beta_{ij}(v)\,,

where the coefficients
f^{\pm}
,
f_{i}^{\pm}
,
f_{i}
,
\widetilde{f}_{i}
and
f_{ij}
are only depended on
f
,
\mathfrak{B}
and
\{e\}_{1\leq j\leq 17}
.

We now decompose the first kinetic equation of
G
in (
3.2
) from two aspects:

(I) We first substitute the identity
G=\mathbb{P}G+\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
into the first kinetic equation on
G
, thus rewriting as

(3.29)

\displaystyle\varepsilon\partial_{t}\mathbb{P}G+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G-(E\cdot v)\sqrt{M}\mathsf{q}_{1}=\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\Psi(\mathbb{P}G)+\Gamma(G,G)\,,

where

(3.30)

\displaystyle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)=-\left(\varepsilon\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathscr{L}+\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}-\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v)\right)\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\,,

and

(3.31)

\displaystyle\Psi(\mathbb{P}G)=-\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}G+\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v)\mathbb{P}G\,.

Based on the definition (
1.15
) of
\mathbb{P}G
, direct calculation implies that the left terms in the equation (
3.29
) is

(3.32)

\displaystyle\varepsilon\partial_{t}\mathbb{P}G+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G-(E\cdot v)\sqrt{M}\mathsf{q}_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\pm}\varepsilon\partial_{t}(\rho^{\pm}-\tfrac{3}{2}\theta)\beta^{\pm}(v)+\sum_{\pm}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\big[\varepsilon\partial_{t}u_{i}+\partial_{i}(\rho^{\pm}-\tfrac{3}{2}\theta)-(\pm E_{i})\big]\beta_{i}^{\pm}(v)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}\big(\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\partial_{t}\theta+\partial_{i}u_{i}\big)\beta_{i}(v)+\sum_{i=1}^{3}\tfrac{1}{2}\partial_{i}\theta\widetilde{\beta}_{i}(v)+\sum_{1\leq i<j\leq 3}(\partial_{i}u_{j}+\partial_{j}u_{i})\beta_{ij}(v)\,,

thus belonging to the space
\textrm{Span}\{\mathfrak{B}\}\subset L^{2}_{v}
. We thereby project the equation (
3.29
) into
\textrm{Span}\{\mathfrak{B}\}
and obtain

(3.33)

\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\beta^{\pm}(v):\ \varepsilon\partial_{t}(\rho^{\pm}-\tfrac{3}{2}\theta)=\Theta^{\pm}+\Psi^{\pm}+\Gamma^{\pm}\,,\\[5.69054pt]
\beta_{i}^{\pm}(v):\ \varepsilon\partial_{t}u_{i}+\partial_{i}(\rho^{\pm}-\tfrac{3}{2}\theta)-(\pm E_{i})=\Theta_{i}^{\pm}+\Psi_{i}^{\pm}+\Gamma_{i}^{\pm}\,,\ 1\leq i\leq 3\,,\\[5.69054pt]
\beta_{i}(v)\ :\ \tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\partial_{t}\theta+\partial_{i}u_{i}=\Theta_{i}+\Psi_{i}+\Gamma_{i}\,,\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\ 1\leq i\leq 3\,,\\[5.69054pt]
\widetilde{\beta}_{i}(v)\ :\ \tfrac{1}{2}\partial_{i}\theta=\widetilde{\Theta}_{i}+\widetilde{\Psi}_{i}+\widetilde{\Gamma}_{i}\,,\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\ \ 1\leq i\leq 3\,,\\[5.69054pt]
\beta_{ij}(v):\partial_{i}u_{j}+\partial_{j}u_{i}=\Theta_{ij}+\Psi_{ij}+\Gamma_{ij}\,,\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\!1\leq i\neq j\leq 3\,,\end{array}\right.

where all the symbols
\Theta
,
\Psi
and
\Gamma
with various indexes are the coefficients of
\mathcal{P}_{\mathfrak{B}}\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)
,
\mathcal{P}_{\mathfrak{B}}\Psi(\mathbb{P}G)
and
\mathcal{P}_{\mathfrak{B}}\Gamma(G,G)
, respectively.

(II) We project the first kinetic equation of
G
in (
3.2
) into
\textrm{Ker}(\mathscr{L})
by multiplying the vectors
\phi_{1}(v)
,
\phi_{2}(v)
,
\tfrac{1}{2}\phi_{3}(v)
,
\tfrac{1}{2}\phi_{4}(v)
,
\tfrac{1}{2}\phi_{5}(v)
and
\tfrac{2}{3}\phi_{6}(v)
, respectively, and integrating over
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
. Thanks to the fact
\Gamma(G,G)\in\textrm{Ker}^{\perp}(\mathscr{L})
shown in Lemma
2.4
, the careful calculation reduces to

(3.34)

\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\varepsilon\partial_{t}\rho^{+}+\mathrm{div}_{x}u=\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\phi_{1}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,,\\[5.69054pt]
\varepsilon\partial_{t}\rho^{-}+\mathrm{div}_{x}u=\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\phi_{2}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,,\\[5.69054pt]
\varepsilon\partial_{t}u_{i}+\partial_{i}\big(\tfrac{\rho^{+}+\rho^{-}}{2}+\theta\big)=\tfrac{1}{2}\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\phi_{i+2}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\ \textrm{for }1\leq i\leq 3\,,\\[5.69054pt]
\varepsilon\partial_{t}\theta+\tfrac{2}{3}\mathrm{div}_{x}u=\tfrac{1}{3}\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\phi_{6}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,.\end{array}\right.

Based on the decompositions (
3.33
) and (
3.34
), we can derive the dissipation
\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}
of the fluid part
\mathbb{P}G
for the integer
s\geq 3
. More precisely, we will give the following proposition.

Proposition 3.3
.

Assume that
(G,E,B)
is the solution to the perturbed VMB system (
1.9
) constructed in Proposition
3.1
. Let integer
s\geq 3
. Then there is positive constant
C>0
, independent of
\varepsilon>0
, such that

(3.35)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathrm{div}_{x}E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\leq-\varepsilon\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathscr{A}_{s}(G)（(t)+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\big(\|G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}\big)\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\big(\|G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}\big)\big((\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big)

for all
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
, where the quantity
\mathscr{A}_{s}(G)(t)
is defined as

(3.36)

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{s}(G)(t)=

\displaystyle\sum_{|m|\leq s-1}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\Big[\left\langle 4\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i},\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}+\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\langle 32\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial_{j}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i}\zeta_{ij}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+\left\langle 32\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\left\langle 4\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\zeta_{i}^{+}(v)+\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\zeta_{i}^{-}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big]\,.

Here the functions
\zeta_{i}^{\pm}(v)
,
\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)
and
\zeta_{ij}(v)
are some fixed linear combinations of the basis
\mathfrak{B}
defined in (
3.22
).

Proof of Proposition
3.3
.

We prove this conclusion by three steps: we first derive the three fluid dissipative terms
\|\nabla_{x}u\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}^{2}
,
\|\nabla_{x}\theta\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}
and
\|\nabla_{x}\rho^{+}\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\rho^{-}\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}
from the hydrodynamics relations (
3.33
) and (
3.34
), where
\rho^{\pm}
,
u
and
\theta
are the three parts of the fluid part
\mathbb{P}G
by three steps. Finally, we combine the estimates obtained in the previous three steps.

Step 1. Bounds on
\|\nabla_{x}u\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}
for the integer
s\geq 3
.
For all multi-indexes
m\in\mathbb{N}^{3}
satisfying
|m|\leq s-1
, we derive from the last
u
-equation and the third
\theta
-equation of (
3.33
) that

(3.37)

\displaystyle-\Delta_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{3}\partial_{j}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i}=-\sum_{j\neq i}\partial_{j}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i}-\partial_{i}\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j\neq i}\partial^{m}_{x}\partial_{j}\big(-\partial_{i}u_{j}+\Theta_{ij}+\Psi_{ij}+\Gamma_{ij}\big)-\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\big(-\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\partial_{t}\theta+\Theta_{i}+\Psi_{i}+\Gamma_{i}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j\neq i}\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}(-\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\partial_{t}\theta+\Theta_{j}+\Psi_{j}+\Gamma_{j})-\sum_{j\neq i}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}_{x}(\Theta_{ij}+\Psi_{ij}+\Gamma_{ij})

\displaystyle-\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}(-\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\partial_{t}\theta+\Theta_{i}+\Psi_{i}+\Gamma_{i})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\partial_{t}\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta+\sum_{\bm{\Lambda}\in\{\Theta,\Psi,\Gamma\}}\Big[\sum_{j\neq i}(\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\bm{\Lambda}_{j}-\partial_{j}\partial^{m}_{x}\bm{\Lambda}_{ij})-\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\bm{\Lambda}_{i}\Big]\,.

Then the definition (
3.28
) of
\mathcal{P}_{\mathfrak{B}}
tells us that there is a certain linear combinations
\zeta_{ij}(v)
of
\mathcal{B}
such that

(3.38)

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{\Lambda}\in\{\Theta,\Psi,\Gamma\}}\Big[\sum_{j\neq i}(\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\bm{\Lambda}_{j}-\partial_{j}\partial^{m}_{x}\bm{\Lambda}_{ij})-\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\bm{\Lambda}_{i}\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{3}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\Psi(\mathbb{P}G)+\Gamma(G,G),\zeta_{ij}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,.

So, we have

(3.39)

\displaystyle-\Delta_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i}=-\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\partial_{t}\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\Psi(\mathbb{P}G)+\Gamma(G,G),\zeta_{ij}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,.

Furthermore, the forth
\theta
-equation of (
3.34
) gives us

(3.40)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\partial_{t}\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta=\tfrac{1}{3}\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u-\tfrac{1}{6}\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\phi_{6}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,.

Then we deduce that

(3.41)

\displaystyle-\Delta_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i}-\tfrac{1}{3}\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u=

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{6}\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\phi_{6}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{3}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\Psi(\mathbb{P}G)+\Gamma(G,G),\zeta_{ij}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,,

which implies that by multiplying
\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i}
, integrating by parts over
x\in\mathbb{T}^{3}
and summing up for
1\leq i\leq 3

(3.42)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\tfrac{1}{3}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{C}_{1}}{\underbrace{-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\sum_{j=1}^{3}\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\partial_{t}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G+\Psi(\mathbb{P}G)+\Gamma(G,G),\zeta_{ij}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial^{m}_{x}\partial_{j}u_{i}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{C}_{2}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{6}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\partial_{t}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G+\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\phi_{6}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial^{m}_{x}\partial_{i}u_{i}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{C}_{3}}{\underbrace{\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\left\langle\varepsilon\left\langle\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\zeta_{ij}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial^{m}_{x}\partial_{j}u_{i}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}\,,

where we utilize the cancellation
\left\langle\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\phi_{6}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}=0
since
\phi_{6}(v)\in\textrm{Ker}(\mathscr{L})
.

We then estimate the terms
\textbf{C}_{1}
,
\textbf{C}_{2}
and
\textbf{C}_{3}
one by one. For the term
\textbf{C}_{1}
, we decompose as

(3.43)

\displaystyle\textbf{C}_{1}=

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{C}_{1}(\Theta)}{\underbrace{-\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\varepsilon\partial_{t}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\zeta_{ij}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial^{m}_{x}\partial_{j}u_{i}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{C}_{1}(\Psi)}{\underbrace{-\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\zeta_{ij}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial^{m}_{x}\partial_{j}u_{i}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}\ \underset{\textbf{C}_{1}(\Gamma)}{\underbrace{-\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Gamma(G,G),\zeta_{ij}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial^{m}_{x}\partial_{j}u_{i}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}\,.

Recalling the expression (
3.30
) of
\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)
, we derive from the integration by parts over
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, the Hölder inequality and the Sobolev embedding theory that

(3.44)

\displaystyle\textbf{C}_{1}(\Theta)=

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}v\zeta_{ij}(v)\partial^{m}_{x}\partial_{j}u_{i}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\mathscr{L}\zeta_{ij}(v)\partial^{m}_{x}\partial_{j}u_{i}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\varepsilon\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}(E\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G),\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\big(\mathsf{q}\nabla_{v}\zeta_{ij}(v)-\tfrac{1}{2}v\mathsf{q}\zeta_{ij}(v)\big)\partial^{m}_{x}\partial_{j}u_{i}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}(B\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G),\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}v\times\nabla_{v}\mathsf{q}\zeta_{ij}(v)\partial^{m}_{x}\partial_{j}u_{i}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Big(\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\|\partial^{m}_{x}(E\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\partial^{m}_{x}(B\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big)\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\big(1+\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\big)\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

for
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
, where the last second inequality is implied by the fact that all functions appearing in the previous estimations involving the function
\zeta_{ij}(v)
and depending only on the variable
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
are in
L^{2}_{v}
(since
\zeta_{ij}(v)
includes a factor
\exp(-\frac{|v|^{2}}{4})
). Similarly, by (
3.31
) and (
1.15
) we have

(3.45)

\displaystyle\textbf{C}_{1}(\Psi)=

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\big[(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}G-\tfrac{1}{2}(E\cdot v)\mathbb{P}G\big],\mathsf{q}\zeta_{ij}(v)\partial^{m}_{x}\partial_{j}u_{i}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\,.

We also derive from the Hölder inequality and Lemma
2.4
that

(3.46)

\displaystyle\textbf{C}_{1}(\Gamma)\leq

\displaystyle C\|G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\partial_{j}u_{i}\zeta_{ij}(v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\left(\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\right)\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\,.

Consequently, we obtain

(3.47)

\displaystyle\textbf{C}_{1}=

\displaystyle\textbf{C}_{1}(\Theta)+\textbf{C}_{1}(\Psi)+\textbf{C}_{1}(\Gamma)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\right)\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\left(1+\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\right)\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

for
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
. We derive from the analogous procedures in estimations of
\textbf{C}_{1}
that

(3.48)

\displaystyle\textbf{C}_{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\right)\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\left(1+\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\right)\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\,.

For the term
\textbf{C}_{3}
, making use of the third equation of (
3.34
) reduces to

(3.49)

\displaystyle\textbf{C}_{3}=

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{j}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i}\zeta_{ij}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{j}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\varepsilon\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i}\zeta_{ij}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{j}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i}\zeta_{ij}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\underset{\textbf{C}_{31}}{\underbrace{-\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{j}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\big(\tfrac{\rho^{+}+\rho^{-}}{2}+\theta\big)\zeta_{ij}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{C}_{32}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{j}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\phi_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\zeta_{ij}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}\,.

The Hölder inequality implies that the term
\textbf{C}_{31}
is bounded by

(3.50)

\displaystyle\textbf{C}_{31}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\|\partial_{j}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\big\|\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\big(\tfrac{\rho^{+}+\rho^{-}}{2}+\theta\big)\big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\zeta_{ij}(v)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\left(\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\right)\,,

where the part (1) of Lemma
2.1
is also utilized in the last inequality. One employs the similar arguments in estimating the term
\textbf{C}_{1}
in (
3.47
) and then yields

(3.51)

\displaystyle\textbf{C}_{32}\leq

\displaystyle C(\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+C(1+\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\,.

We summarize the above estimates and know

(3.52)

\displaystyle\textbf{C}_{3}\leq-\varepsilon\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{j}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i}\zeta_{ij}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+C\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\left(\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\right)

\displaystyle+C(\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+C(1+\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\,.

As a result, substituting the bounds (
3.47
), (
3.48
) and (
3.52
) into the equality (
3.42
) yields that

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\tfrac{1}{3}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq-\varepsilon\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{j}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i}\zeta_{ij}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\left(\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C(\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+C(1+\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\left(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\right)\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\left(1+\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\right)\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\,,

which implies by Young’s inequality

(3.53)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq

\displaystyle-3\varepsilon\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{j}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i}\zeta_{ij}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C(\|G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})\|\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{C}{\varepsilon}(1+\|G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\delta(\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}})

for any small
\delta>0
to be determined and for all
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
.

Step 2. Bounds on
\|\nabla_{x}\theta\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}
for the integer
s\geq 3
.
For any multi-index
m\in\mathbb{N}^{3}
with
|m|\leq s-1
, we derive from the last second
\partial_{i}\theta
-equation of (
3.33
) that

(3.54)

\displaystyle-\Delta_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta=-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\partial_{i}\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta=-2\sum_{i=1}^{3}\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}(\tfrac{1}{2}\partial_{i}\theta)=-2\sum_{i=1}^{3}\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}(\widetilde{\Theta}_{i}+\widetilde{\Psi}_{i}+\widetilde{\Gamma}_{i})\,.

By the definition (
3.28
) of
\mathcal{P}_{\mathfrak{B}}
we know that there are some certain linear combinations
\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)

(i=1,2,3)
of
\mathfrak{B}
such that

\displaystyle-2\sum_{i=1}^{3}\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}(\widetilde{\Theta}_{i}+\widetilde{\Psi}_{i}+\widetilde{\Gamma}_{i})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{3}\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\left[\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G),\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}+\left\langle\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}+\left\langle\Gamma(G,G),\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right]\,,

which tells us

(3.55)

\displaystyle-\Delta_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta=\sum_{i=1}^{3}\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\left[\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G),\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}+\left\langle\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}+\left\langle\Gamma(G,G),\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right]\,.

We now take
L^{2}_{x}
-inner product by multiplying
\partial^{m}_{x}\theta
in the above equality and integrating by parts over
x\in\mathbb{T}^{3}
. Consequently, we have

(3.56)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}=\underset{\textbf{D}_{1}}{\underbrace{\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G),\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle+\underset{\textbf{D}_{2}}{\underbrace{\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}+\underset{\textbf{D}_{3}}{\underbrace{\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Gamma(G,G),\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}\,.

We next deal with the terms
\textbf{D}_{1}
,
\textbf{D}_{2}
and
\textbf{D}_{3}
term by term. The definition (
3.30
) of
\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)
yields that the quantity
\textbf{D}_{1}
can be decomposed as

(3.57)

\displaystyle\textbf{D}_{1}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\Big\langle\varepsilon\partial^{m}_{x}\partial_{t}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathscr{L}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G

\displaystyle+\partial^{m}_{x}[\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G]-\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\partial^{m}_{x}[\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v)\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G],\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\varepsilon\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\underset{\textbf{D}_{11}}{\underbrace{-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\varepsilon\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{D}_{12}}{\underbrace{-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\mathscr{L}\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}+\underset{\textbf{D}_{13}}{\underbrace{\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\varepsilon\partial^{m}_{x}(E\mathsf{q}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G),\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\nabla_{v}(\tfrac{\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)}{\sqrt{M}})\sqrt{M}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{D}_{14}}{\underbrace{-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle v\cdot\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}+\underset{\textbf{D}_{15}}{\underbrace{\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}[(v\times B)\mathsf{q}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G],\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\nabla_{v}\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}\,,

where we make use of integration by parts over
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
in the terms
\textbf{D}_{13}
and
\textbf{D}_{15}
. Noticing that the last
\theta
-equation in (
3.34
)

\varepsilon\partial_{t}\theta+\tfrac{2}{3}\mathrm{div}_{x}u=\tfrac{1}{3}\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\phi_{6}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,,

the quantity
\textbf{D}_{11}
can be rewritten as

(3.58)

\displaystyle\textbf{D}_{11}=

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{D}_{111}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{2}{3}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}\cdot\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v),\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{D}_{112}}{\underbrace{-\tfrac{1}{3}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\cdot\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v),\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G),\phi_{6}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{D}_{113}}{\underbrace{-\tfrac{1}{3}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\cdot\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v),\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\phi_{6}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}\,.

One observes that
\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)
and
\phi_{6}(v)
are both in
L^{2}_{v}
. By utilizing the Hölder inequality and the part (1) of Lemma
2.1
, the quantity
\textbf{D}_{111}
is bounded by

(3.59)

\displaystyle\textbf{D}_{111}\leq C\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\,.

Since
\phi_{6}(v)\in\textrm{Ker}(\mathscr{L})
, we derive from (
3.30
), the Hölder inequality, the Sobolev embedding theory and the part (1) of Lemma
2.1
that

\displaystyle\textbf{D}_{112}=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{3}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\Big\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\cdot\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v),\partial^{m}_{x}\big\langle v\cdot\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G-\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v)\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\phi_{6}(v)\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{3}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\Big\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\cdot\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v),\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle v\cdot\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\phi_{6}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\varepsilon E\cdot v,\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1}\sqrt{M(v)}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

(3.60)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(1+\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}

for
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
, where the second equality is implied by making use of the relation

\left\langle\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G-\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v)\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\phi_{6}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}=-\left\langle\varepsilon E\cdot v,\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1}\sqrt{M(v)}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,.

Indeed, recalling that
\phi_{6}(v)=(\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}-\frac{3}{2})\sqrt{M(v)}\mathsf{q}_{2}
for
\mathsf{q}_{2}=[1,1]
, by utilizing the integration by parts over
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
and the fact
(v\times B)\cdot v=0
we have

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G-\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v)\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\phi_{6}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G-\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v)\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,(\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}-\frac{3}{2})\sqrt{M(v)}\mathsf{q}_{2}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,-v\sqrt{M(v)}\mathsf{q}_{1}+(\tfrac{|v|^{2}}{2}-\tfrac{3}{2})\sqrt{M(v)}\mathsf{q}_{1}\tfrac{v}{2}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle-\left\langle\tfrac{\varepsilon}{2}(E\cdot v)\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,(\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}-\frac{3}{2})\sqrt{M(v)}\mathsf{q}_{1}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left\langle\varepsilon E\cdot v,\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1}\sqrt{M(v)}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,.

By employing the similar arguments in estimation of the term
\textbf{D}_{112}
, we deduce from (
3.31
), the Hölder inequality, the Sobolev embedding theory and the part (1) of Lemma
2.1
that

(3.61)

\textbf{D}_{113}\leq C\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\,.

In summary, we have

(3.62)

\displaystyle\textbf{D}_{11}=

\displaystyle\textbf{D}_{111}+\textbf{D}_{112}+\textbf{D}_{113}\leq C\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C(1+\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+C\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+C\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}\left(\|\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\right)

for any small
\delta>0
to be determined and for
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
. For the term
\textbf{D}_{12}
, we derive from the Hölder inequality and the part (1) of Lemma
2.1
that

(3.63)

\displaystyle\textbf{D}_{12}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathscr{L}\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\,.

Moreover, the term
\textbf{D}_{13}
is bounded by

(3.64)

\displaystyle\textbf{D}_{13}\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon\sum_{i=1}^{3}\|\partial^{m}_{x}(E\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla_{v}(\tfrac{\mathsf{q}\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)}{\sqrt{M}})\sqrt{M}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\partial^{m}_{x}(E\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\,,

where we make use of the Hölder inequality, the Sobolev embedding theory and the part (1) of Lemma
2.1
. For the term
\textbf{D}_{14}
, we have

(3.65)

\displaystyle\textbf{D}_{14}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|v\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\,.

By the similar estimation of the term
\textbf{D}_{13}
, the quantity
\textbf{D}_{15}
is bounded by

(3.66)

\displaystyle\textbf{D}_{15}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}\|\partial^{m}_{x}(B\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|v\nabla_{v}(\mathsf{q}\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v))\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\partial^{m}_{x}(B\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\,.

Consequently, collecting the above bounds yields

(3.67)

\displaystyle\textbf{D}_{1}=

\displaystyle\varepsilon\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\textbf{D}_{11}+\textbf{D}_{12}+\textbf{D}_{13}+\textbf{D}_{14}+\textbf{D}_{15}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+C\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}\left(\|\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}(\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}+\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})+C\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+\delta\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

for any small
\delta>0
to be determined and for
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
.

For the term
\textbf{D}_{2}
, it is derived from (
3.31
), the definition (
1.15
) of
\mathbb{P}G
, the Hölder inequality and the Sobolev embedding theory that

(3.68)

\displaystyle\textbf{D}_{2}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\big[-\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}G+\tfrac{1}{2}\mathsf{q}\varepsilon(E\cdot v)\mathbb{P}G\big],\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\partial^{m}_{x}[(\varepsilon E+B)\mathbb{P}G]\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\varepsilon\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\,.

For the quantity
\textbf{D}_{3}
, we derive from Lemma
2.4
, the composition
G=\mathbb{P}G+\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
and the definition (
1.15
) of
\mathbb{P}G
that

(3.69)

\displaystyle\textbf{D}_{3}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\Gamma(G,G),\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{i=1}^{3}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}(\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)})\,.

We finally plug the bounds of
\textbf{D}_{1}
,
\textbf{D}_{2}
and
\textbf{D}_{3}
into the relation (
3.56
) and obtain the bound

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\delta\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+C\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}\left(\|\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}(\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}+\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})+C\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big(\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)\big(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\big)\,,

which immediately yields by Young’s inequality that for all mulit-indexes
m\in\mathbb{N}^{3}
with
|m|\leq s-1

(3.70)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+2\delta\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C(\|G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})(\|\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)})

for any small
\delta>0
to be determined and for all
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
.

Step 3. Bounds on
\|\nabla_{x}\rho^{\pm}\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}
for the integer
s\geq 3
.
From the second
u_{i}
-equation of (
3.33
), we deduce

(3.71)

\displaystyle-\Delta_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{\pm}\pm\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}E=\sum_{i=1}^{3}\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}(-\partial_{i}\rho^{\pm}\pm E_{i})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}(-\varepsilon\partial_{t}u_{i}+\tfrac{3}{2}\partial_{i}\theta+\Theta_{i}^{\pm}+\Psi_{i}^{\pm}+\Gamma_{i}^{\pm})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\Big[-\varepsilon\partial_{t}u_{i}+\tfrac{3}{2}\partial_{i}\theta+\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G),\zeta^{\pm}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+\left\langle\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\zeta^{\pm}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}+\left\langle\Gamma(G,G),\zeta^{\pm}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\Big]

for some certain linear combinations
\zeta^{\pm}_{i}(v)
of the basis
\mathfrak{B}
. Multiplying by
\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{\pm}
, taking
L^{2}_{x}
-inner and integrating by parts over
x\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
give us

(3.72)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{\pm}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\pm\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}E,\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{\pm}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}(-\varepsilon\partial_{t}u_{i}+\tfrac{3}{2}\partial_{i}\theta+\Theta_{i}^{\pm}+\Psi_{i}^{\pm}+\Gamma_{i}^{\pm}),\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{\pm}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}\,.

Noticing that the equation
\mathrm{div}_{x}E=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1}\sqrt{M}\mathrm{d}v
in the perturbed VMB system (
3.2
) reduces to

(3.73)

\mathrm{div}_{x}E=\rho^{+}-\rho^{-}\,,

we then sum up for the indexes
\pm
in (
3.72
) and obtain

(3.74)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{\gamma=\pm}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}(-\varepsilon\partial_{t}u_{i}+\tfrac{3}{2}\partial_{i}\theta+\Theta_{i}^{\gamma}+\Psi_{i}^{\gamma}+\Gamma_{i}^{\gamma}),\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{\gamma}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{E}_{1}}{\underbrace{\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\varepsilon\partial_{t}\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i},\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}\ \underset{\textbf{E}_{2}}{\underbrace{-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G),\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\zeta^{+}_{i}(v)+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\zeta^{-}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{E}_{3}}{\underbrace{-\tfrac{3}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\partial_{i}\theta,\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}\ \underset{\textbf{E}_{4}}{\underbrace{-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\zeta^{+}_{i}(v)+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\zeta^{-}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{E}_{5}}{\underbrace{-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\Gamma(G,G),\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\zeta^{+}_{i}(v)+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\zeta^{-}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}\,.

Then we will estimate the quantities
\textbf{E}_{i}
for
i=1,2,\cdots,5
. For the term
\textbf{E}_{1}
, we deduce from the first two
\rho^{\pm}
-equations of (
3.34
) that

(3.75)

\displaystyle\textbf{E}_{1}=

\displaystyle\varepsilon\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i},\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i},\varepsilon\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}+\varepsilon\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\varepsilon\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i},\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}+2\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{E}_{11}}{\underbrace{-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i},\partial^{m}_{x}\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)(\phi_{1}(v)+\phi_{2}(v))\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{E}_{12}}{\underbrace{-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i},\partial^{m}_{x}\Psi(\mathbb{P}G)(\phi_{1}(v)+\phi_{2}(v))\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}\,.

Since
\nabla_{v}(\phi_{1}(v)+\phi_{2}(v))=-\frac{1}{2}v(\phi_{1}(v)+\phi_{2}(v))
, we have

(3.76)

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\phi_{1}(v)+\phi_{2}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left\langle\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\nabla_{v}(\phi_{1}(v)+\phi_{2}(v))\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\left\langle\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,v(\phi_{1}(v)+\phi_{2}(v))\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v)\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\phi_{1}(v)+\phi_{2}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,,

where the fact
(v\times B)\cdot v=0
is utilized. Since
\phi_{1}(v)
,
\phi_{2}(v)
are both in
\textrm{Ker}(\mathscr{L})
, the previous identity and (
3.30
) reduce to

(3.77)

\displaystyle\textbf{E}_{11}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}\Big\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}[v\cdot\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G+\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v)\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G](\phi_{1}(v)+\phi_{2}(v))\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i},(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)(\phi_{1}(v)+\phi_{2}(v))\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|v(\phi_{1}(v)+\phi_{2}(v))\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\,.

The cancellation (
3.76
) still holds when the function
\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
is replaced by
\mathbb{P}G
. Namely,

(3.78)

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}G,\phi_{1}(v)+\phi_{2}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}=\left\langle\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v)\mathbb{P}G,\phi_{1}(v)+\phi_{2}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,.

Then by the relation (
3.31
) of
\Psi(\mathbb{P}G)
we compute the term
\textbf{E}_{12}
that

\displaystyle\textbf{E}_{12}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i},\partial^{m}_{x}[\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}G-\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v)\mathbb{P}G](\phi_{1}(v)+\phi_{2}(v))\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i},\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}G,\phi_{1}(v)+\phi_{2}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

(3.79)

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i},\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v)\mathbb{P}G,\phi_{1}(v)+\phi_{2}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}=0\,.

Then we, consequently, obtain

(3.80)

\displaystyle\textbf{E}_{1}\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i},\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}+2\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+C\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i},\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}+3\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+C\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\,,

where the last inequality is derived from the Young’s inequality and the part (1) of Lemma
2.1
.

Next, we estimate the term
\textbf{E}_{2}
. From the relation (
3.30
), we deduce

(3.81)

\displaystyle\textbf{E}_{2}

\displaystyle=\underset{\textbf{E}_{21}}{\underbrace{\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\varepsilon\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\partial_{t}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\zeta^{+}_{i}(v)+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\zeta^{-}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{E}_{22}}{\underbrace{-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle v\cdot\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\zeta^{+}_{i}(v)+\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\zeta^{-}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{E}_{23}}{\underbrace{-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\mathscr{L}\zeta^{+}_{i}(v)+\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\mathscr{L}\zeta^{-}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{E}_{24}}{\underbrace{-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}(\varepsilon E\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G),\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\nabla_{v}(\tfrac{\mathsf{q}\zeta^{+}_{i}(v)}{\sqrt{M}})\sqrt{M}+\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\nabla_{v}(\tfrac{\mathsf{q}\zeta^{-}_{i}(v)}{\sqrt{M}})\sqrt{M}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{E}_{25}}{\underbrace{-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}(B\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G),\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}v\times\nabla_{v}(\tfrac{\mathsf{q}\zeta^{+}_{i}(v)}{\sqrt{M}})\sqrt{M}+\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}v\times\nabla_{v}(\tfrac{\mathsf{q}\zeta^{-}_{i}(v)}{\sqrt{M}})\sqrt{M}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}\,,

where the last two terms is derived from the integration by parts over
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
. For the term
\textbf{E}_{21}
, we derive from the first two
\rho^{\pm}
-equations of (
3.34
) that

\displaystyle\textbf{E}_{21}=

\displaystyle\varepsilon\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\zeta_{i}^{+}(v)+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\zeta^{-}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\varepsilon\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\zeta^{+}_{i}(v)+\varepsilon\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\zeta^{-}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\varepsilon\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\zeta_{i}^{+}(v)+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\zeta^{-}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+\underset{\textbf{E}_{211}}{\underbrace{\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u(\zeta_{i}^{+}(v)+\zeta_{i}^{-}(v))\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{E}_{212}}{\underbrace{-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\phi_{1}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\zeta^{+}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

(3.82)

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{E}_{213}}{\underbrace{-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\phi_{2}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\zeta^{-}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}\,.

The Hölder inequality implies that the term
\textbf{E}_{211}
is bounded by

(3.83)

\displaystyle\textbf{E}_{211}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}\|\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\zeta^{+}_{i}(v)+\zeta^{-}_{i}(v)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\,.

Since
\nabla_{v}\phi_{i}(v)=-\tfrac{1}{2}v\phi_{i}(v)
for
i=1,2
, we have

(3.84)

\displaystyle\left\langle-\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}H,\phi_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}=\left\langle\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)H,\nabla_{v}\phi_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left\langle\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v)H,\phi_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}+\left\langle\mathsf{q}[(v\times B)\cdot\tfrac{1}{2}v]H,\phi_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left\langle\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v)H,\phi_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}

for any
H=[H^{+},H^{-}]
, where the last equality is derived from the relation
(v\times B)\cdot v=0
. If taking
H=\mathbb{P}G
or
\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
, we obtain

(3.85)

\displaystyle\left\langle-\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}G+\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v)\mathbb{P}G,\phi_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}=0\,,

\displaystyle\left\langle-\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G+\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v)\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\phi_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}=0\,.

Additionally,
\phi_{1}(v)
is in
\textrm{Ker}(\mathscr{L})
, we deduce from (
3.30
) and (
3.31
) that

(3.86)

\displaystyle\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\phi_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}=-\left\langle v\cdot\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\phi_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}

for
i=1,2
. Thus we have

(3.87)

\displaystyle\textbf{E}_{212}+\textbf{E}_{213}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle v\cdot\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\phi_{1}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\zeta^{+}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle v\cdot\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\phi_{2}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\zeta^{-}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}\|\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\|v\phi_{1}(v)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\zeta^{+}_{i}(v)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}+\|v\phi_{2}(v)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\zeta^{-}_{i}(v)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\,.

Consequently, the previous bounds give us

(3.88)

\displaystyle\textbf{E}_{21}\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\zeta_{i}^{+}(v)+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\zeta^{-}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+C\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\,.

For the quantity
\textbf{E}_{22}
, it is derived from the Hölder inequality that

(3.89)

\displaystyle\textbf{E}_{22}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}(\|\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{x}})

\displaystyle\times(\|v\zeta^{+}_{i}(v)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}+\|v\zeta^{-}_{i}(v)\|_{L^{2}_{v}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}(\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{x}})\,.

Similarly, for the term
\textbf{E}_{23}
we have

(3.90)

\displaystyle\textbf{E}_{23}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}(\|\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{x}})

\displaystyle\times(\|\mathscr{L}\zeta^{+}_{i}(v)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathscr{L}\zeta^{-}_{i}(v)\|_{L^{2}_{v}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}(\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{x}})\,.

The term
\textbf{E}_{24}
can be estimated as

(3.91)

\displaystyle\textbf{E}_{24}\leq
