\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}\|\partial^{m}_{x}(\varepsilon E\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}(\|\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{c}})

\displaystyle\times\big(\|\nabla_{v}(\tfrac{\mathsf{q}\zeta_{i}^{+}(v)}{\sqrt{M}})\sqrt{M}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}+\|\nabla_{v}(\tfrac{\mathsf{q}\zeta_{i}^{-}(v)}{\sqrt{M}})\sqrt{M}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|\partial^{m}_{x}(E\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)

by making use of the Hölder inequality and the Sobolev embedding theory. Similarly, for the term
\textbf{E}_{25}
we have

(3.92)

\displaystyle\textbf{E}_{25}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}\|\partial^{m}_{x}(B\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}(\|\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{c}})

\displaystyle\times\big(\|v\times\nabla_{v}(\tfrac{\mathsf{q}\zeta_{i}^{+}(v)}{\sqrt{M}})\sqrt{M}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}+\|v\times\nabla_{v}(\tfrac{\mathsf{q}\zeta_{i}^{-}(v)}{\sqrt{M}})\sqrt{M}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\partial^{m}_{x}(B\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)\,.

Therefore, by the bounds on quantities
\textbf{E}_{2i}
for
1\leq i\leq 5
in the previous, we obtain the bound of
\textbf{E}_{2}

(3.93)

\displaystyle\textbf{E}_{2}=

\displaystyle\textbf{E}_{21}+\textbf{E}_{22}+\textbf{E}_{23}+\textbf{E}_{24}+\textbf{E}_{25}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\zeta_{i}^{+}(v)+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\zeta^{-}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+C\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C(\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}+\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)

for
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
.

For the term
\textbf{E}_{3}
, we just employ the Hölder inequality to estimate

(3.94)

\textbf{E}_{3}\leq\tfrac{3}{2}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)\,.

We can deal with the quantity
\textbf{E}_{4}
by utilizing the analogous arguments in estimating the term
\textbf{E}_{2}
(in fact, it is simpler than the estimation of
\textbf{E}_{2}
). More precisely,

(3.95)

\displaystyle\textbf{E}_{4}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}(\varepsilon E\mathbb{P}G),\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\nabla_{v}(\tfrac{\mathsf{q}\zeta^{+}_{i}(v)}{\sqrt{M}})\sqrt{M}+\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\nabla_{v}(\tfrac{\mathsf{q}\zeta^{-}_{i}(v)}{\sqrt{M}})\sqrt{M}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}(B\mathbb{P}G),\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}v\times\nabla_{v}(\tfrac{\mathsf{q}\zeta^{+}_{i}(v)}{\sqrt{M}})\sqrt{M}+\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}v\times\nabla_{v}(\tfrac{\mathsf{q}\zeta^{-}_{i}(v)}{\sqrt{M}})\sqrt{M}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}\varepsilon\|\partial^{m}_{x}(E\mathbb{P}G)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\big(\|\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)

\displaystyle\times\big(\|\nabla_{v}(\tfrac{\mathsf{q}\zeta_{i}^{+}(v)}{\sqrt{M}})\sqrt{M}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}+\|\nabla_{v}(\tfrac{\mathsf{q}\zeta_{i}^{-}(v)}{\sqrt{M}})\sqrt{M}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}\|\partial^{m}_{x}(B\mathbb{P}G)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\big(\|\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)

\displaystyle\times\big(\|v\times\nabla_{v}(\tfrac{\mathsf{q}\zeta_{i}^{+}(v)}{\sqrt{M}})\sqrt{M}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}+\|v\times\nabla_{v}(\tfrac{\mathsf{q}\zeta_{i}^{-}(v)}{\sqrt{M}})\sqrt{M}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\varepsilon\|\partial^{m}_{x}(E\mathbb{P}G)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\partial^{m}_{x}(B\mathbb{P}G)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\big)\big(\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\varepsilon\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)\,.

We finally estimate the term
\textbf{E}_{5}
by employing Lemma
2.4
. The details are shown as

(3.96)

\displaystyle\textbf{E}_{5}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\Gamma(G,G),\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\zeta_{i}^{+}(v)+\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\zeta_{i}^{-}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{i=1}^{3}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\zeta_{i}^{+}(v)+\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\zeta_{i}^{-}(v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}(\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)})(\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{x}})\,.

Collecting the previous estimations on the quantities
\textbf{E}_{1}
,
\textbf{E}_{2}
,
\textbf{E}_{3}
,
\textbf{E}_{4}
,
\textbf{E}_{5}
, and employing the part (1) of Lemma
2.1
or Lemma
2.2
, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\Big(\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i},\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}+\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\zeta_{i}^{+}(v)+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\zeta_{i}^{-}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big)

\displaystyle+3\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+C\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+C\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+\big(\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)\Big[\tfrac{3}{2}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle+C(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})(\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)})\Big]

for
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
, which immediately implies by the Young’s inequality that for all multi-indexes
m\in\mathbb{N}^{3}
with
|m|\leq s-1
(
s\geq 3
)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\varepsilon\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\Big(\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i},\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}+\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\zeta_{i}^{+}(v)+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\zeta_{i}^{-}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big)

\displaystyle+8\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+6\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}

(3.97)

\displaystyle+C(\|G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})(\|\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)})

for
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
.

Step 4. Summarization for the dissipation of the fluid part
\mathbb{P}G
.
We summarize the estimates derived in the previous three steps. We first add
\frac{1}{16}
times of the bound (
3.2
) to the summation of bounds (
3.53
) and (
3.70
). Then we deduce that for all multi-indexes
m\in\mathbb{N}^{3}
with
|m|\leq s-1
(
s\geq 3
)

(3.98)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{16}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\tfrac{1}{16}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\tfrac{1}{16}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{2}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\Big[\left\langle\tfrac{1}{8}\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i},\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\langle\partial_{j}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i}\zeta_{ij}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\left\langle\tfrac{1}{8}\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\zeta_{i}^{+}(v)+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\zeta_{i}^{-}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big]

\displaystyle+C(\|G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})\big(\|\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+2\delta\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\delta\big(\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)

holds for any small
\delta>0
to be determined and for all
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
. We now take
\delta=\tfrac{1}{32}
, so that

(3.99)

\displaystyle 2\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+3\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathrm{div}_{x}E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\Big[\left\langle 4\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i},\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\langle 32\partial_{j}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i}\zeta_{ij}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+\left\langle 32\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\widetilde{\zeta}_{i}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\left\langle 4\partial_{i}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\zeta_{i}^{+}(v)+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\zeta_{i}^{-}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+C(\|G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})\big(\|\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)

holds for all multi-indexes
m\in\mathbb{N}^{3}
with
|m|\leq s-1
(
s\geq 3
) and for all
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
.

Recalling that

(3.100)

\mathbb{P}G=\rho^{+}\phi_{1}(v)+\rho^{-}\phi_{2}(v)+\sum_{i=1}^{3}u_{i}\phi_{i+2}(v)+\theta\phi_{6}(v),

we directly compute that for all
m\in\mathbb{N}^{3}

(3.101)

\displaystyle\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{v}}=

\displaystyle(\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+})^{2}\|\phi_{1}(v)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{v}}+(\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-})^{2}\|\phi_{2}(v)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{3}(\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i})^{2}\|\phi_{i+2}(v)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{v}}+(\partial^{m}_{x}\theta)^{2}\|\phi_{6}(v)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+})^{2}+(\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-})^{2}+2|\partial^{m}_{x}u|^{2}+3(\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+})^{2}\,,

where we utilize the facts
\left\langle 1,M\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}=\left\langle v_{i}^{2},M\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}=1
for
i=1,2,3
and
\left\langle|v|^{4},M\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}=15
. Consequently, we have

(3.102)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}=\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+2\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+3\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\,.

Moreover, one also has

(3.103)

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}=\|\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\rho^{+}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\rho^{-}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+2\|u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+3\|\theta\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\|\rho^{+}-(\rho^{+})_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+2\|\rho^{-}-(\rho^{-})_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+4\|u-(u)_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+6\|\theta-(\theta)_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+2|\mathbb{T}^{3}|\big((\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+2(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+3(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big)+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|\nabla_{x}\rho^{+}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\rho^{-}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+2\|\nabla_{x}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+3\|\nabla_{x}\theta\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}})+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+C\big((\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+2(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+3(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+C\big((\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+2(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+3(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big)

for
s\geq 3
, where the last second inequality is implied by the Poincaré inequality. From substituting the relations (
3.102
) and (
3.103
) into the bound (
3.99
) and summing up for all
|m|\leq s-1
, we deduce that the inequality (
3.35
) holds for all
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
. The proof of Proposition
3.3
is completed.
∎

3.3.
Estimations on some average quantities

In this subsection, we will estimate the average quantities
(\rho^{\pm})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}
,
(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}
and
(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}
appearing in the right-hand side of (
3.35
) by utilizing the conservation laws (
1.20
) of mass, momentum and energy for the VMB system (
1.4
). More precisely, we will give the following proposition.

Proposition 3.4
.

Under the assumptions in Proposition
3.3
and the initial data of
(G,E,B)
satisfying the conservation laws (
1.20
), we have

(3.104)

\displaystyle(\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\leq C\left(\|E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|B\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{x}}\right)\left(\|E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\right)

for some positive constant
C>0
and for all
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
.

The term
\|E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}
will be a part of the energy dissipation as shown in the next subsection and the quantity
\|E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|B\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{x}}
can be controlled by the energy term. As a consequence, the average quantity
(\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}
will be well dealt in deriving the global in times energy bounds uniformly in
\varepsilon
with small initial data. We next prove this proposition.

Proof of Proposition
3.4
.

From the conservation laws (
1.20
) and the definition (
1.19
) of
\rho^{\pm}
,
u
and
\theta
, we deduce

(3.105)

\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}(\rho^{\pm})_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}=0\,,\\[8.53581pt]
(u)_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}=-\frac{1}{2|\mathbb{T}^{3}|}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}E\times B\mathrm{d}x\,,\\[8.53581pt]
(\theta)_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}=-\frac{\varepsilon}{3|\mathbb{T}^{3}|}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}(|E|^{2}+|B-\bar{B}|^{2})\mathrm{d}x\,.\end{array}\right.

Recalling the relation (
1.8
), which means
\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}(B-\bar{B})\mathrm{d}x=0
, we derive from the Poincaré inequality that

(3.106)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|B-\bar{B}|^{2}\mathrm{d}x\leq C\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

for some positive constant
C>0
. Thus, the third relation of (
3.105
) gives us

(3.107)

\displaystyle|(\theta)_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|\leq C(\|E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}})

for all
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
. For the second equality of (
3.105
), it is yielded by the Hölder inequality that

(3.108)

\displaystyle|(u)_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|\leq C\|E\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\,.

Consequently, we obtain

(3.109)

\displaystyle(\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\|B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+C\big(\|E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|B\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{x}}\right)\left(\|E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\right)\,.

The proof of Proposition
3.4
is finished.
∎

3.4.
Decay structures on the Maxwell system

In this subsection, we will find enough dissipation or decay properties on the electronic field
E
and the magnetic field
B
by making use of the Maxwell equations, hence the last four equations in (
3.2
)

(3.110)

\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\partial_{t}E-\nabla_{x}\times B=-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}(u^{+}-u^{-})\,,\\[5.69054pt]
\partial_{t}B+\nabla_{x}\times E=0\,,\\[5.69054pt]
\mathrm{div}_{x}E=\rho^{+}-\rho^{-}\,,\ \mathrm{div}_{x}B=0\,,\end{array}\right.

where we make use of the definition (
1.19
). It is noticed that the second Faraday’s law equation in (
3.110
) does not have explicit dissipative term. If we take
\partial_{t}
on the evolution of the magnetic field
B
and combine with the evolution of
E
, we have

\partial_{tt}B+\nabla_{x}\times(\nabla_{x}\times B)=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\nabla\times(u^{+}-u^{-})\,,

which implies that

(3.111)

\partial_{tt}B-\Delta_{x}B=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\nabla\times(u^{+}-u^{-})

by the equality
\nabla_{x}\times(\nabla_{x}\times B)=-\Delta_{x}B
under the divergence-free property
\mathrm{div}_{x}B=0
.

However, the dissipation of (
3.111
) is remain not enough. We try to derive the Ohm’s law from the microscopic equation of
G
in (
3.2
), which will supply a decay term
\partial_{t}B
. More precisely, we dot with
\mathsf{q}_{1}
in the first equation of (
3.2
), and then we gain

\displaystyle\varepsilon\partial_{t}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1})+v\cdot\nabla_{x}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1})+(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2})-2E\cdot v\sqrt{M}

(3.112)

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}(\mathscr{L}G)\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1}=\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon(E\cdot v)(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2})+\Gamma(G,G)\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1}\,,

where we make use of the relation
\mathsf{q}\mathsf{q}_{1}=\mathsf{q}_{2}
. Recalling the definition (
1.10
) of
\mathscr{L}
and (
1.11
) of
\mathcal{L}
, we calculate

(3.113)

\displaystyle(\mathscr{L}G)\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1}=

\displaystyle[\mathcal{L}G^{+}+\mathcal{L}(G^{+},G^{-}),\mathcal{L}G^{-}+\mathcal{L}(G^{-},G^{+})]\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(G^{+}-G^{-})+\mathcal{L}(G^{+},G^{-})-\mathcal{L}(G^{-},G^{+})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1})-\big[\mathcal{Q}(G^{+},1)+\mathcal{Q}(1,G^{-})-\mathcal{Q}(G^{-},1)-\mathcal{Q}(1,G^{+})\big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1})-\big[\mathcal{Q}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},1)-\mathcal{Q}(1,G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1})\big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1})+\mathfrak{L}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1})\,,

where the linear operator
\mathfrak{L}
is defined as

(3.114)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{L}g=

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(g,-g)=-\big[\mathcal{Q}(g,\sqrt{M})-\mathcal{Q}(\sqrt{M},g)\big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{M}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big(\tfrac{g}{\sqrt{M}}-\tfrac{g_{*}}{\sqrt{M_{*}}}-\tfrac{g^{\prime}}{\sqrt{M^{\prime}}}+\tfrac{g_{*}^{\prime}}{\sqrt{M_{*}^{\prime}}}\Big)|v-v_{*}|M_{*}\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,.

Then the equation (
3.4
) of
G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1}
can be rewritten as

(3.115)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}(\mathcal{L}+\mathfrak{L})(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1})=

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\partial_{t}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1})-v\cdot\nabla_{x}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1})-(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2})

\displaystyle+2E\cdot v\sqrt{M}+\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon(E\cdot v)(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2})+\Gamma(G,G)\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1}\,.

We now display the following properties of the operator
\mathfrak{L}
, so that we can derive the corresponding macroscopic form from (
3.115
).

Lemma 3.1
.

The linear operator
\mathfrak{L}
has the following properties:

(1)

Hilbert’s decomposition of
\mathfrak{L}
:

The linear operator
\mathfrak{L}
can be decomposed as

(3.116)

\mathfrak{L}g=\nu(v)g-\mathfrak{K}g\,,

where
\mathfrak{K}
is a compact integral operator on
L^{2}_{v}
.

(2)

Coercivity of
\mathfrak{L}
:

The linear operator
\mathfrak{L}
is a nonnegative self-adjoint operator on
L^{2}_{v}
with null space

(3.117)

\mathrm{Ker}(\mathfrak{L})=\mathrm{Span}\{\sqrt{M}\}\,.

Moreover, the following coercivity estimate holds: there is
C>0
such that, for all
g\in\mathrm{Ker}^{\perp}(\mathfrak{L})\subset L^{2}_{v}
,

(3.118)

\|g\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\leq C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g\mathfrak{L}g\mathrm{d}v\,.

In particular, for any
g\in\mathrm{Ker}^{\perp}(\mathfrak{L})\subset L^{2}_{v}
,

(3.119)

\|g\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\leq C\|\mathfrak{L}g\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,.

(3)

Properties of
\mathcal{L}+\mathfrak{L}
:

For
\Phi(v)=v\sqrt{M}=[\chi_{2}(v),\chi_{3}(v),\chi_{4}(v)]\in L^{2}_{v}
and
\Psi(v)=(\tfrac{|v|^{2}}{2}-\tfrac{3}{2})\sqrt{M}=\chi_{5}(v)\in L^{2}_{v}
, there are two functions
\widetilde{\Phi}
,
\widetilde{\Psi}\in\mathrm{Ker}^{\perp}(\mathcal{L}+\mathfrak{L})=\mathrm{Ker}^{\perp}(\mathfrak{L})
such that

(3.120)

(\mathcal{L}+\mathfrak{L})\widetilde{\Phi}=\Phi\ \textrm{and }(\mathcal{L}+\mathfrak{L})\widetilde{\Psi}=\Psi\,,

which are uniquely determined in
\mathrm{Ker}^{\perp}(\mathcal{L}+\mathfrak{L})
. Furthermore, there exist two scalar valued functions
\alpha,\beta:\mathbb{R}^{+}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
such that

(3.121)

\widetilde{\Phi}(v)=\alpha(|v|)\Phi(v)\quad\textrm{and}\quad\widetilde{\Psi}(v)=\beta(|v|)\Psi(v)\,.

Remark 3.1
.

The relations (
3.121
) imply that

(3.122)

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Phi_{i}(v)\widetilde{\Phi}_{j}(v)\mathrm{d}v=\tfrac{1}{2}\sigma\delta_{ij}\,,

where
\sigma=\tfrac{2}{3}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Phi\cdot\widetilde{\Phi}\mathrm{d}v
defines the electrical conductivity
\sigma>0
. Moreover, we also define the energy conductivity
\lambda>0
by
\lambda=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Psi\widetilde{\Psi}\mathrm{d}v
.

Proof of Lemma
3.1
.

The first two parts of Lemma
3.1
can be seen in Proposition 5.8 and 5.9 of Arsénio-Saint-Raymond’s book
[
5
]
, while the last part of Lemma
3.1
and Remark
3.1
have been shown on Page 46 in
[
5
]
. So we omit the details of the proof here.
∎

Next we multiply by
\widetilde{\Phi}(v)
in (
3.115
) and integrate over
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
. Then we derive from (
3.115
), the part (3) of Lemma
3.1
and Remark
3.1
that

(3.123)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}(u^{+}-u^{-})=

\displaystyle\sigma E-\varepsilon\left\langle\partial_{t}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}-\left\langle v\cdot\nabla_{x}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left\langle-(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2})+\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon(E\cdot v)(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2})+\Gamma(G,G)\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\overset{\Delta}{=}

\displaystyle\sigma E+\mathcal{K}(G,E,B)\,,

where we utilize the relation

\displaystyle\left\langle(\mathcal{L}+\mathfrak{L})(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}=\left\langle G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},(\mathcal{L}+\mathfrak{L})\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}=\left\langle G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\Phi\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}=u^{+}-u^{-}

implied by the definition (
1.19
) and the self-adjoint property of
\mathcal{L}+\mathfrak{L}
.
Then, from substituting (
3.123
) into (
3.111
) we deduce that

(3.124)

\partial_{tt}B-\Delta_{x}B+\sigma\partial_{t}B=\nabla_{x}\times\mathcal{K}(G,E,B)\,,

where we use the Faraday’s law equation
\partial_{t}B+\nabla_{x}\times E=0
. We thereby have found the decay term
\partial_{t}B
of
B
-equation. Moreover, by plugging the relation (
3.123
) into (
3.110
), we get

(3.125)

\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\partial_{t}E-\nabla_{x}\times B+\sigma E=-\mathcal{K}(G,E,B)\,,\\[5.69054pt]
\partial_{t}B+\nabla_{x}\times E=0\,,\\[5.69054pt]
\mathrm{div}_{x}E=\rho^{+}-\rho^{-}\,,\ \mathrm{div}_{x}B=0\,,\end{array}\right.

in which we have the damping structure
\sigma E
of the electric field
E
.

Based on the equation (
3.124
) and Maxwell system (
3.125
), we derive the following proposition, which gives us some energy dissipative structures on the electric field
E
and magnetic field
B
.

Proposition 3.5
.

Assume that
(G,E,B)
is the solution to the perturbed VMB system (
1.9
) constructed in Proposition
3.1
. Then there is a constant
C>0
, independent of
\varepsilon>0
, such that the energy inequality on
(E,B)

(3.126)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathscr{E}_{1}(E,B)

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathscr{A}_{s}(E,B)(t)+\mathscr{D}_{1}(E,B)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\big(\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\big(|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C(\varepsilon+\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\big((\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big)

holds for any
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
and integer
s\geq 3
, where

(3.127)

\displaystyle\mathscr{E}_{1}(E,B)=

\displaystyle\delta_{2}\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}}+\delta_{2}\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\delta_{1}(\sigma-1)\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+\delta_{1}\|\partial_{t}B+B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+(1-\delta_{1})\|\partial_{t}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}\,,

and

(3.128)

\displaystyle\mathscr{D}_{1}(E,B)=

\displaystyle\tfrac{\sigma}{2}\delta_{2}\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\tfrac{\sigma}{2}\|\partial_{t}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+\tfrac{\delta_{1}}{4}\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}

for some small
\delta_{1},\delta_{2}>0
, independent of
\varepsilon\in(0,1]
, and the constant
\sigma>0
is mentioned in Remark
3.1
. Here the quantity
\mathscr{A}_{s}(E,B)(t)
is defined as

(3.129)

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{s}(E,B)(t)=\sum_{|m|\leq s-1}\Big[\tfrac{\varepsilon}{2}\delta_{2}\big\|\big\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{v}}-\delta_{2}\big\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\cdot\partial^{m}_{x}E\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big]

\displaystyle+\sum_{|m|\leq s-2}\Big[\tfrac{\varepsilon}{2}\big\|\nabla_{x}\times\big\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\big\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B+\delta_{1}\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}\Big]\,.

Remark 3.2
.

As shown in the proof, the smallness of the constants
\delta_{1},\delta_{2}>0
is such that the energy dissipative parts in the left-hand side of (
3.183
) have the lower bound
\mathscr{D}_{1}(G,B)
and the coefficient
1-\delta_{1}
of
\|\partial_{t}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}
in the energy functional
\mathscr{E}_{1}(E,B)
is lager than
\tfrac{1}{2}
. However, the energy functional
\mathscr{E}_{1}(E,B)
may not be nonnegative, because the chosen constants
\delta_{1},\delta_{2}>0
can not ensure the positivity of
\delta_{2}-\delta_{1}+\delta_{1}\sigma
, so that the term
(\delta_{2}-\delta_{1}+\delta_{1}\sigma)\|B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}
in the following equality

(3.130)

\displaystyle\delta_{2}\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\delta_{1}(\sigma-1)\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\delta_{2}-\delta_{1}+\delta_{1}\sigma)\|B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+(\delta_{2}+1)\sum_{|m|=s-2}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+(\delta_{2}+1-\delta_{1}+\delta_{1}\sigma)\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-3}_{x}}

may be negatively valued. Thanks to the energy inequality (
3.3
) in Proposition
3.2
, we will design a new positive energy after carefully adjusting the coefficients of
\mathscr{E}_{1}(E,B)
.

Proof of Proposition
3.5
.

We will complete this proof by two steps: We first derive a energy inequality which involves the dissipative structures
\|\partial_{t}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}
and
\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}
of the magnetic field
B
by utilizing the second order wave system (
3.124
). Secondly, from the first order wave system (
3.125
), we can derive another energy inequality involving the dissipative structure
\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}
of the electric field
E
.

Step 1. Derivations of the dissipative structures
\|\partial_{t}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}
and
\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}
.
By acting the derivative operator
\partial^{m}_{x}
on the equation (
3.124
) for all multi-indexes
m\in\mathbb{N}^{3}
with
|m|\leq s-2

(s\geq 3)
, taking the
L^{2}
-inner product by dot with
\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B
and integrating by parts over
x\in\mathbb{T}^{3}
, we deduce that

(3.131)

\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}(\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}})+\sigma\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}=\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}\mathcal{K}(G,E,B),\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}\,.

If we replace the multiplied vector
\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B
by the vector
\partial^{m}_{x}B
in the previous process, we have

(3.132)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B+\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}-\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+(\sigma-1)\|\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)

\displaystyle-\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}\mathcal{K}(G,E,B),\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

for all
|m|\leq s-2
, where we utilize the equality

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{tt}\partial^{m}_{x}B,\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}=-\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B+\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}-\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+(\sigma-1)\|\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)\,.

Then, the equality (
3.131
) being added by
\delta_{1}
times of (
3.132
) yields

(3.133)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(\delta_{1}\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B+\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+(1-\delta_{1})\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\delta_{1}(\sigma-1)\|\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)

\displaystyle+(\sigma-\delta_{1})\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\delta_{1}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}\mathcal{K}(G,E,B),\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}+\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\mathcal{K}(G,E,B),\delta_{1}\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

holds for all
|m|\leq s-2\ (s\geq 3)
, where
\delta_{1}\in(0,1]
is a small number to be determined.

Next, we estimate the two terms in the right-hand side of the equality (
3.133
). Recalling the definition of
\mathcal{K}(G,E,B)
in (
3.123
), we decompose the first term in the right-hand side of (
3.133
) as some parts. More precisely,

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}\mathcal{K}(G,E,B),\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{F}_{1}}{\underbrace{-\varepsilon\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\partial_{t}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{F}_{2}}{\underbrace{-\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{F}_{3}}{\underbrace{-\varepsilon\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle E\cdot\nabla_{v}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle+\underset{\textbf{F}_{4}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle(E\cdot v)(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{F}_{5}}{\underbrace{-\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle(v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

(3.134)

\displaystyle+\underset{\textbf{F}_{6}}{\underbrace{\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Gamma(G,G)\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

holds for all
|m|\leq s-2\ (s\geq 3)
. The terms
\textbf{F}_{i}

(i=1,2,\cdots,6)
will be controlled one by one.

For the term
\textbf{F}_{1}
, we derive from the equation (
3.124
) that

(3.135)

\displaystyle\textbf{F}_{1}=

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{F}_{11}}{\underbrace{-\varepsilon\left\langle\nabla_{x}\big[\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big],\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{F}_{12}}{\underbrace{-\varepsilon\sigma\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle+\underset{\textbf{F}_{13}}{\underbrace{\varepsilon\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}\mathcal{K}(G,E,B)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}\,.

It is derived from the Hölder inequality and the decomposition
G=\mathbb{P}G+\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
that the quantity
\textbf{F}_{11}
is bounded by

(3.136)

\displaystyle\textbf{F}_{11}\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}(\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}G)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\mathsf{q}_{1}\widetilde{\Phi}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big)\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\,.

Similarly, the quantity
\textbf{F}_{12}
is controlled by

(3.137)

\displaystyle\textbf{F}_{12}\leq C\varepsilon\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big)\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\,.

The calculations on
\textbf{F}_{13}
are rather tedious, since the expression
\mathcal{K}(G,E,B)
includes six parts. We plug the expression
\mathcal{K}(G,E,B)
defined in (
3.123
) into the term
\textbf{F}_{13}
. Then we get

\displaystyle\textbf{F}_{13}=

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\varepsilon^{2}}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big\|\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{F}_{131}}{\underbrace{-\varepsilon\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{F}_{132}}{\underbrace{-\varepsilon^{2}\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle E\cdot\nabla_{v}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle+\underset{\textbf{F}_{133}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon^{2}\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle(E\cdot v)(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{F}_{134}}{\underbrace{-\varepsilon\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle(v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

(3.138)

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{F}_{135}}{\underbrace{-\varepsilon\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\Gamma(G,G)\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}\,.

We estimate

(3.139)

\displaystyle\textbf{F}_{131}\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\nabla_{x}(\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}G)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\mathsf{q}_{1}\widetilde{\Phi}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|v\mathsf{q}_{1}\widetilde{\Phi}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}})\,,

where the Hölder inequality and the decomposition
G=\mathbb{P}G+\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
are utilized. The quantity
\textbf{F}_{132}
is bounded by

(3.140)

\displaystyle\textbf{F}_{132}=

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{2}\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle E(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2}),\nabla_{v}\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}(EG)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\mathsf{q}_{1}\widetilde{\Phi}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\mathsf{q}_{2}\nabla_{v}\widetilde{\Phi}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{x}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big(\|E\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}G\|_{L^{4}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq|m^{\prime}|\leq|m|+1}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\sum_{|m^{\prime}|\leq|m|}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}G\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{x}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon^{2}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big)\,,

where we make use of the Hölder inequality, the Sobolev embeddings
H^{1}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{4}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
,
H^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{\infty}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
and the decomposition
G=\mathbb{P}G+\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
. Similarly, the term
\textbf{F}_{133}
can be controlled by

(3.141)

\displaystyle\textbf{F}_{133}\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big)\,.

Furthermore, we estimate the term
\textbf{F}_{134}
for
|m|\leq s-2\ (s\geq 3)
as

(3.142)

\displaystyle\textbf{F}_{134}=

\displaystyle\varepsilon\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle(v\times B)(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2}),\nabla_{v}\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}(BG)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\mathsf{q}_{1}\widetilde{\Phi}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|v\mathsf{q}_{2}\nabla_{v}\widetilde{\Phi}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big(\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|G\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq|m^{\prime}|\leq|m|+1}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}G\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\sum_{|m^{\prime}|\leq|m|}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}B\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{H^{s-2}_{x}}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\nabla_{x}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon(\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}})\big(\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big)

by utilizing the decomposition
G=\mathbb{P}G+\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
, the Hölder inequality and the Sobolev embedding
H^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{\infty}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
. In order to control the quantity
\textbf{F}_{135}
, we employ Lemma
2.4
and the decomposition
G=\mathbb{P}G+\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
. We thereby get

\displaystyle\textbf{F}_{135}=

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\left\langle\left\langle\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}G\times\widetilde{\Phi},\mathsf{q}_{1}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\mathsf{q}_{1},\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\Gamma(G,G)\times\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big\|\left\langle\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}G\times\widetilde{\Phi},\mathsf{q}_{1}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\mathsf{q}_{1}\widetilde{\Phi}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|\nabla_{x}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big)

\displaystyle\times\left(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+(\rho^{+})_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+|(u)_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|+(\theta)_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\right)

(3.143)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\left(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+(\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\right)\,,

where the last inequality is implied by the Young’s inequality and the part (1) of Lemma
2.1
or Lemma
2.2
.

Collecting the estimates on the quantities
\textbf{F}_{13i}

(i=1,2,\cdots,5)
in the previous, we obtain the bounds of
\textbf{F}_{13}

(3.144)

\displaystyle\textbf{F}_{13}\leq

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\varepsilon^{2}}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big\|\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+C\varepsilon\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})

\displaystyle\times\big(\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big((\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big)

holds for all
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
, where Lemma
2.2
is utilized. Then we derive from the bounds of
\textbf{F}_{11}
,
\textbf{F}_{12}
,
\textbf{F}_{13}
and Lemma
2.2
that the term
\textbf{F}_{1}
is controlled by

(3.145)

\displaystyle\textbf{F}_{1}\leq

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\varepsilon^{2}}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big\|\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}-\varepsilon\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)(\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})

\displaystyle\times\big(\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big((\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big)

for any
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
, where we employ Lemma
2.2
.

For the term
\textbf{F}_{2}
, we deduce from the Hölder inequality, the the decomposition
G=\mathbb{P}G+\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
and the part (1) of Lemma
2.1
that

(3.146)

\displaystyle\textbf{F}_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}(\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}G)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|v\mathsf{q}_{1}\widetilde{\Phi}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla_{x}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)})\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\,.

For the terms
\textbf{F}_{3}
and
\textbf{F}_{4}
, it is yielded by the similar arguments in estimating the term
\textbf{F}_{132}
that

(3.147)

\displaystyle\textbf{F}_{3}+\textbf{F}_{4}\leq C\varepsilon\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\,.

We also deduce the bound of the term
\textbf{F}_{5}

(3.148)

\displaystyle\textbf{F}_{5}\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon(\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}})\big(\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big)

by employing the analogous arguments in estimating the term
\textbf{F}_{134}
.

For the term
\textbf{F}_{6}
, by utilizing Lemma
2.4
and the decomposition
G=\mathbb{P}G+\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
, we get

(3.149)

\displaystyle\textbf{F}_{6}\leq

\displaystyle C\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathsf{q}_{1}\widetilde{\Phi}\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\times\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+|(\rho^{+})_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|+|(\rho^{-})_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|+|(u)_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|+|(\theta)_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|\big)\,.

We summarize the estimates on
F_{i}

(i=1,2,\cdots,6)
, and obtain

(3.150)

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}\mathcal{K}(G,E,B),\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\varepsilon^{2}}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big\|\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}-\varepsilon\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)(\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\Big[\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\Big]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big((\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\Big[\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+|(\rho^{+})_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|+|(\rho^{-})_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|+|(u)_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|+|(\theta)_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|\Big]

for all
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
and any multi-indexes
