with
|m|\leq s-2

(s\geq 3)
.

Next, we estimate the quantity
\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\mathcal{K}(G,E,B),\delta_{1}\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}
. By the definition of
\mathcal{K}(G,E,B)
in (
3.123
), we decompose this term as

(3.151)

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\mathcal{K}(G,E,B),\delta_{1}\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{G}_{1}}{\underbrace{-\varepsilon\left\langle\partial_{t}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\delta_{1}\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{G}_{2}}{\underbrace{-\left\langle\left\langle v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\delta_{1}\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{G}_{3}}{\underbrace{-\varepsilon\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle E\cdot\nabla_{v}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\delta_{1}\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle+\underset{\textbf{G}_{4}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle(E\cdot v)(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\delta_{1}\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{G}_{5}}{\underbrace{-\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle(v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\delta_{1}\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle+\underset{\textbf{G}_{6}}{\underbrace{\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Gamma(G,G)\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\delta_{1}\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}\,.

For the term
\textbf{G}_{1}
, we have

(3.152)

\displaystyle\textbf{G}_{1}=

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\delta_{1}\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\delta_{1}\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}\,,

where the last term can be controlled by

(3.153)

\displaystyle\varepsilon\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\delta_{1}\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon\delta_{1}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\mathsf{q}_{1}\widetilde{\Phi}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\delta_{1}\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big)\,.

Here we make use of the Hölder inequality and the decomposition
G=\mathbb{P}G+\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
. Then, we obtain the bound of
\textbf{G}_{1}

(3.154)

\displaystyle\textbf{G}_{1}\leq

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\delta_{1}\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\delta_{1}\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big)

for all
|m|\leq s-2

(s\geq 3)
and for small
\delta_{1}\in(0,1]
to be determined. Similar calculations on the inequality (
3.153
) reduce to

(3.155)

\displaystyle\textbf{G}_{2}\leq C\varepsilon\delta_{1}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big)\,.

Via the analogous arguments of the estimates on the term
\textbf{F}_{132}
,
\textbf{F}_{133}
and
\textbf{F}_{134}
, one can get the bounds

(3.156)

\displaystyle\textbf{G}_{3}+\textbf{G}_{4}\leq C\varepsilon\delta_{1}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

and

(3.157)

\displaystyle\textbf{G}_{5}\leq C\varepsilon\delta_{1}(\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}})\big(\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big)\,.

We also deduce from the same operations as estimating the term
\textbf{F}_{6}
that

(3.158)

\displaystyle\textbf{G}_{6}\leq

\displaystyle C\delta_{1}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\times\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+|(\rho^{+})_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|+|(\rho^{-})_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|+|(u)_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|+|(\theta)_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|\big)\,.

Via the summarization of the bounds
\textbf{G}_{i}

(i=1,2,\cdots,6)
, we derive

(3.159)

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\mathcal{K}(G,E,B),\delta_{1}\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq-\varepsilon\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\delta_{1}\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\delta_{1}(\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}})(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\delta_{1}(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})(\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\delta_{1}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big((\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big)

for all
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
and for small
\delta_{1}\in(0,1]
to be determined.

Consequently, via substituting the bounds (
3.150
) and (
3.159
) into the equality (
3.133
), summing up for
|m|\leq s-2

(s\geq 3)
and utilizing the Young’s inequality, we yield that

(3.160)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(\delta_{1}\|\partial_{t}B+B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+(1-\delta_{1})\|\partial_{t}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+\delta_{1}(\sigma-1)\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}\big)

\displaystyle+(\sigma-\tfrac{3\delta_{1}}{2})\|\partial_{t}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+\tfrac{\delta_{1}}{2}\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{|m|\leq s-2}\Big[\tfrac{\varepsilon}{2}\big\|\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+\Big\langle\nabla_{x}\times\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B+\delta_{1}\partial^{m}_{x}B\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}\Big]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\big[\|\partial_{t}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\tfrac{C\varepsilon}{\delta_{1}}(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)})+C\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big((\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big)

for all
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
and for small
\delta_{1}\in(0,1]
to be determined.

Step 2. Derivations of the dissipative structure
\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}
.
We take the derivative operator
\partial^{m}_{x}
on the first two equation of (
3.125
), multiply by
\partial^{m}_{x}E
and
\partial^{m}_{x}B
respectively, and integrate by parts over
x\in\mathbb{T}^{3}
for all
|m|\leq s-1

(s\geq 3)
. We then deduce that

(3.161)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)+\sigma\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}B,\partial^{m}_{x}E\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}-\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}E,\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}-\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\mathcal{K}(G,E,B),\partial^{m}_{x}E\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\mathcal{K}(G,E,B),\partial^{m}_{x}E\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}\,,

where we make use of the cancellation
\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}B,\partial^{m}_{x}E\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}-\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}E,\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}=0
. So, we only need to control the term
-\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\mathcal{K}(G,E,B),\partial^{m}_{x}E\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}
for all multi-indexes
m\in\mathbb{N}^{3}
with
|m|\leq s-1

(s\geq 3)
.

Recalling the definition of
\mathcal{K}(G,E,B)
in (
3.123
), we have

(3.162)

\displaystyle-\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\mathcal{K}(G,E,B),\partial^{m}_{x}E\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{H}_{1}}{\underbrace{\varepsilon\left\langle\partial_{t}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial^{m}_{x}E\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}+\underset{\textbf{H}_{2}}{\underbrace{\left\langle\left\langle v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial^{m}_{x}E\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle+\underset{\textbf{H}_{3}}{\underbrace{\varepsilon\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle E\cdot\nabla_{v}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial^{m}_{x}E\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}\ \underset{\textbf{H}_{4}}{\underbrace{-\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle(E\cdot v)(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial^{m}_{x}E\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle+\underset{\textbf{H}_{5}}{\underbrace{\varepsilon\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle(v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial^{m}_{x}E\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}\ \underset{\textbf{H}_{6}}{\underbrace{-\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Gamma(G,G)\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial^{m}_{x}E\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}\,.

For the term
\textbf{H}_{1}
, we deduce from the first equation of (
3.125
) and the definition of
\mathcal{K}(G,E,B)
in (
3.123
) that

(3.163)

\displaystyle\textbf{H}_{1}=

\displaystyle\varepsilon\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\cdot\partial^{m}_{x}E\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}-\varepsilon\left\langle\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}E\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\varepsilon\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\cdot\partial^{m}_{x}E\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\underset{\textbf{H}_{11}}{\underbrace{-\varepsilon\left\langle\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{H}_{12}}{\underbrace{\varepsilon\left\langle\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\sigma\partial^{m}_{x}E\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}+\underset{\textbf{H}_{13}}{\underbrace{\varepsilon\left\langle\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial^{m}_{x}\mathcal{K}(G,E,B)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}\,.

If
|m|\leq s-2
, the Hölder inequality, the Poincaré inequality and the decomposition
G=\mathbb{P}G+\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
reduce to

(3.164)

\displaystyle\textbf{H}_{11}\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon\|\partial^{m}_{x}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\mathsf{q}_{1}\widetilde{\Phi}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\nabla_{x}\times\partial^{m}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{H^{s-2}_{x}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big)

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{H^{s-2}_{x}}\big(|(\rho^{+})_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}|+|(\rho^{-})_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}|+|(u)_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}|+|(\theta)_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}|\big)\,.

If
|m|=s-1
, by, additionally, integrating by parts over
x\in\mathbb{T}^{3}
, we have

(3.165)

\displaystyle\textbf{H}_{11}\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{H^{s-2}_{x}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big)\,.

In summary, for all multi-indexes
m\in\mathbb{N}^{3}
with
|m|\leq s-1
, the following bound holds:

(3.166)

\displaystyle\textbf{H}_{11}\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{H^{s-2}_{x}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big)

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{H^{s-2}_{x}}\big(|(\rho^{+})_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}|+|(\rho^{-})_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}|+|(u)_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}|+|(\theta)_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}|\big)\,.

Via the similar calculations of
\textbf{H}_{11}
in the case
|m|\leq s-2
, we have

(3.167)

\displaystyle\textbf{H}_{12}\leq

\displaystyle C\sigma\varepsilon\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big)

\displaystyle+C\sigma\varepsilon\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big(|(\rho^{+})_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}|+|(\rho^{-})_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}|+|(u)_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}|+|(\theta)_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}|\big)

holds for any
|m|\leq s-1
.

Again from utilizing the definition of
\mathcal{K}(G,E,B)
in (
3.123
), we derive

(3.168)

\displaystyle\textbf{H}_{13}=

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\varepsilon^{2}}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big\|\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{H}_{131}}{\underbrace{-\varepsilon\left\langle\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\left\langle v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{H}_{132}}{\underbrace{-\varepsilon^{2}\left\langle\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle E\cdot\nabla_{v}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle+\underset{\textbf{H}_{133}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon^{2}\left\langle\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle(E\cdot v)(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{\textbf{H}_{134}}{\underbrace{-\varepsilon\left\langle\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle(v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}(G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2}),\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle+\underset{\textbf{H}_{135}}{\underbrace{\varepsilon\left\langle\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Gamma(G,G)\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}}}\,.

The Hölder inequality reduces to

(3.169)

\displaystyle\textbf{H}_{131}\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+(\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big)\,,

where the last inequality is implied by making use of the decomposition
G=\mathbb{P}G+\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
, the Poincaré inequality and the Young’s inequality. Via the analogous arguments of the estimates on the term
\textbf{F}_{132}
and
\textbf{F}_{133}
, we can control the terms
\textbf{H}_{132}
and
\textbf{H}_{133}
in the case
|m|\leq s-1
with
m\neq 0

(3.170)

\displaystyle\textbf{H}_{132}+\textbf{H}_{133}\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big)\,.

If
m=0
, the quantity
\textbf{H}_{132}+\textbf{H}_{133}
can be estimated as

(3.171)

\displaystyle\textbf{H}_{132}+\textbf{H}_{133}=

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{2}\left\langle\left\langle G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\left\langle E\cdot\nabla_{v}(\tfrac{\widetilde{\Phi}}{\sqrt{M}})\sqrt{M},G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{2}\|G\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|E\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathsf{q}_{1}\widetilde{\Phi}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|\mathsf{q}_{2}\nabla_{v}(\tfrac{\widetilde{\Phi}}{\sqrt{M}})\sqrt{M}\big\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon^{2}\|G\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\nabla_{x}G\|^{\frac{3}{4}}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|E\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon^{2}\|G\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|E\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon^{2}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big)\,,

where we utilize the Sobolev interpolation inequality
\|f\|_{L^{4}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})}\leq C\|f\|^{\frac{1}{4}}_{L^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})}\|\nabla_{x}f\|^{\frac{3}{4}}_{L^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})}
. We thereby have

(3.172)

\displaystyle\textbf{H}_{132}+\textbf{H}_{133}\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big)

for all
|m|\leq s-1
. Similarly, one can easily estimate

(3.173)

\displaystyle\textbf{H}_{134}\leq C\varepsilon\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+(\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big)

for all
|m|\leq s-1
. For the term
\textbf{H}_{135}
, we derive from Lemma
2.4
, Lemma
2.2
, the decomposition of
G=\mathbb{P}G+\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
and the Poincaré inequality that

(3.174)

\displaystyle\textbf{H}_{135}\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+(\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big)

for any
|m|\leq s-1
. Collecting the all estimates in the previous, we obtain

(3.175)

\displaystyle\textbf{H}_{13}\leq

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\varepsilon^{2}}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big\|\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+(\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\varepsilon(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\varepsilon(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\big((\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big)\,,

where we use the Young’s inequality and Lemma
2.2
.

We plug the estimates on the bounds of quantities
\textbf{H}_{11}
,
\textbf{H}_{12}
and
\textbf{H}_{13}
into the expression of
\textbf{H}_{1}
, which then gives us

(3.176)

\displaystyle\textbf{H}_{1}\leq

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big[\tfrac{\varepsilon}{2}\big\|\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}-\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\cdot\partial^{m}_{x}E\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\big(\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\big((\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big)+\tfrac{\sigma}{4}\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\varepsilon(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\varepsilon(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\big((\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big)

for all
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
and
|m|\leq s-1
. Here we also make use of the Young’s inequality and Lemma
2.2
.

From the decomposition
G=\mathbb{P}G+\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
and the Hölder inequality, we deduce that

(3.177)

\displaystyle\textbf{H}_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|v\mathsf{q}_{1}\widetilde{\Phi}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{\sigma}{4}\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+C\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)

for all
|m|\leq s-1
. Similarly, we have

(3.178)

\displaystyle\textbf{H}_{3}+\textbf{H}_{4}=

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle E\cdot\nabla_{v}(\tfrac{\widetilde{\Phi}}{\sqrt{M}})\sqrt{M},G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{2}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial^{m}_{x}E\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\,.

For the term
\textbf{H}_{5}
, we have

(3.179)

\displaystyle\textbf{H}_{5}\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}G\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon\|B\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\|\partial^{m}_{x}G\|_{L^{4}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{H^{s-2}_{x}}\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{\frac{3}{4}}_{L^{2}_{x}}\|B\|^{\frac{1}{4}}_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}G\|^{\frac{3}{4}}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|G\|^{\frac{1}{4}}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big)

for all
|m|\leq s-1
, where we utilize the Hölder inequality, the Young’s inequality, the Sobolev embedding
H^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{\infty}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
and the Sobolev interpolation inequality
\|f\|_{L^{4}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})}\leq C\|f\|^{\frac{1}{4}}_{L^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})}\|\nabla_{x}f\|^{\frac{3}{4}}_{L^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})}
. For the term
\textbf{H}_{6}
, it is easily derived from Lemma
2.4
, the decomposition
G=\mathbb{P}G+\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
and the Young’s inequality that

(3.180)

\displaystyle\textbf{H}_{6}\leq

\displaystyle C\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathsf{q}_{1}\widetilde{\Phi}\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\Big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\sum_{\bm{\gamma}\in\{\rho^{\pm},u,\theta\}}|(\bm{\gamma})_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big(\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)

\displaystyle+C\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big((\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big)

for all
|m|\leq s-1
.

Consequently, we substitute the bounds (
3.176
), (
3.177
), (
3.178
), (
3.179
) and (
3.180
) into the equality (
3.162
), which leads to

(3.181)

\displaystyle-\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}\mathcal{K}(G,E,B),\partial^{m}_{x}E\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big[\tfrac{\varepsilon}{2}\big\|\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}-\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\cdot\partial^{m}_{x}E\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\tfrac{\sigma}{2}\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+C\big(\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\varepsilon(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\varepsilon(1+\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\big((\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big)

for all
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
and for any
|m|\leq s-1
. Finally, plugging the inequality (
3.181
) into the relation (
3.161
) and summing up for all
|m|\leq s-1
reduce to

(3.182)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}}\big)+\tfrac{\sigma}{2}\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{|m|\leq s-1}\Big[\tfrac{\varepsilon}{2}\big\|\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}-\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\cdot\partial^{m}_{x}E\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\big(\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\varepsilon(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\varepsilon(1+\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\big((\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big)

for any
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
.

Let
\delta_{2}\in(0,1]
be a small constant to be determined. Then, adding the
\delta_{2}
times of (
3.182
) to (
3.160
) tells us

(3.183)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big(\delta_{2}\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}}+\delta_{2}\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\delta_{1}(\sigma-1)\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+\delta_{1}\|\partial_{t}B+B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+(1-\delta_{1})\|\partial_{t}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\tfrac{\sigma}{2}\delta_{2}\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+(\sigma-\tfrac{3}{2}\delta_{1})\|\partial_{t}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+(\tfrac{\delta_{1}}{2}-C\delta_{2})\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{|m|\leq s-2}\Big[\tfrac{\varepsilon}{2}\big\|\nabla_{x}\times\big\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{x}\times\big\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}B+\delta_{1}\partial^{m}_{x}B\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}\Big]

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{|m|\leq s-1}\Big[\tfrac{\varepsilon}{2}\delta_{2}\big\|\big\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{v}}-\delta_{2}\big\langle\partial^{m}_{x}G\cdot\mathsf{q}_{1},\widetilde{\Phi}\cdot\partial^{m}_{x}E\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big]

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon(1+\tfrac{1}{\delta_{1}})\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)

\displaystyle+C(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\big(\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}\big)

\displaystyle+C(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\big(|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)

\displaystyle+C(\varepsilon+\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\big((\rho^{+})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\rho^{-})^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(u)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}+(\theta)^{2}_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big)

for any
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
and integer
s\geq 3
, where the small constants
\delta_{1},\delta_{2}\in(0,1]
are to be determined. We first take
\delta_{1}\in(0,\min\{\tfrac{1}{2},\tfrac{1}{3}\sigma\}
such that

(3.184)

1-\delta_{1}\geq\tfrac{1}{2}>0\ \ \textrm{and }\ \sigma-\tfrac{3}{2}\delta_{1}\geq\tfrac{1}{2}\sigma>0\,,

and then choose
\delta_{2}\in(0,\min\{1,\tfrac{1}{4C}\delta_{1}\}]
such that

(3.185)

\tfrac{\delta_{1}}{2}-C\delta_{2}\geq\tfrac{\delta_{1}}{4}>0\,,

where the constant
C>0
is mentioned as in the inequality (
3.183
) and independent of
\varepsilon\in(0,1]
. Then the inequality (
3.183
) implies (
3.126
) and the proof of Proposition
3.5
is finished.
∎

3.5.
Summarizations

In this subsection, we will summarize the all energy estimates derived from the previous four subsections. We first choose a constant
\eta_{0}\in(0,1]（
, independent of
\varepsilon>0
, such that for all
\eta\in(0,\eta_{0}]

(3.186)

\displaystyle\lambda-C\eta\geq\tfrac{\lambda}{2}>0\,,

where the constant
C>0
is mentioned as in Proposition
3.3
. Combining the Young’s inequality, the bound (
3.103
) and the inequality (
3.104
) in Proposition
3.4
, we add the
\eta
times of the relation (
3.35
) to the bound (
3.3
) and then deduce that

(3.187)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(\|G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}\big)+\varepsilon\eta\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathscr{A}_{s}(G)(t)

\displaystyle+\tfrac{\lambda}{2\varepsilon^{2}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+\eta\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\eta\|\mathrm{div}_{x}E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|G\|^{4}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|E\|^{4}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|^{4}_{H^{s}_{x}}\big)

\displaystyle\times\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\big)

\displaystyle+C\big(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\big)\sum_{|m|\leq s-1}\|\nabla_{v}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

holds for all
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
,
\eta\in(0,\eta_{0}]
and integer
s\geq 3
.

We next take a constant
\eta_{1}\in(0,1]
, independent of
\varepsilon>0
, such that for all
\eta\in(0,\eta_{0}]

(3.188)

\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}1-\eta\eta_{1}\delta_{1}\geq 1-\eta_{0}\eta_{2}\delta_{1}\geq\tfrac{1}{2}\,,\\[5.69054pt]
\tfrac{\lambda}{2}-C\eta\eta_{1}\geq\tfrac{\lambda}{2}-C\eta_{0}\eta_{1}\geq\tfrac{\lambda}{4}\,,\\[5.69054pt]
\eta-C\eta\eta_{1}\geq\tfrac{\eta}{2}\,,\end{array}\right.

where the positive constant
C
is given in Proposition
3.5
. Then
\eta_{1}
can be assumed as

\displaystyle\eta_{1}=\min\{1,\tfrac{1}{2C},\tfrac{1}{2\eta_{0}\delta_{1}},\tfrac{\lambda}{4C\eta_{0}}\}\in(0,1]\,.

We now multiply the inequality (
3.126
) by
\eta_{1}\eta
and then add it to the bound (
3.187
). We finally derive from the Young’s inequality and the estimate (
3.104
) in Proposition
3.4
that for all
0<\varepsilon\leq 1

(3.189)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(\|G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\eta_{1}\eta\mathscr{E}_{1}(E,B)\big)+\varepsilon\eta\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(\mathscr{A}_{s}(G)(t)+\eta_{1}\mathscr{A}_{s}(E,B)(t)\big)

\displaystyle\big(\tfrac{\lambda}{2}-C\eta_{1}\eta\big)\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+(\eta-C\eta_{1}\eta)\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\eta\|\mathrm{div}_{x}E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\eta_{1}\eta\mathscr{D}_{1}(E,B)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|G\|^{4}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|E\|^{4}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|^{4}_{H^{s}_{x}}\big)

\displaystyle\times\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}\big)

\displaystyle+C\big(\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\big)\sum_{|m|\leq s-1}\|\nabla_{v}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

holds for any
\eta\in(0,\eta_{0}]
and integer
s\geq 3
.

For notational simplicity, we define the following functionals:

(3.190)

\displaystyle\mathscr{E}_{\eta}(G,E,B)=\|G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\eta_{1}\eta\mathscr{E}_{1}(E,B)\,,

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{s}(G,E,B)(t)=\mathscr{A}_{s}(G)(t)+\eta_{1}\mathscr{A}_{s}(E,B)(t)\,,

\displaystyle\mathscr{D}_{\eta}(G,E,B)=\tfrac{\lambda}{4\varepsilon^{2}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+\tfrac{\eta}{2}\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\eta_{1}\eta\mathscr{D}_{1}(E,B)\,,

\displaystyle\mathscr{D}_{w}(G)=\sum_{|m|\leq s-1}\|\nabla_{v}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\,.

We emphasize that the coefficients relations (
3.188
) ensure that

(3.191)

\displaystyle\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}-\eta_{1}\eta\delta_{1}\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+(\tfrac{1}{2}-\eta_{1}\eta\delta_{1})\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{s-1\leq|m|\leq s}\|\partial^{m}_{x}B\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}\geq 0

for all
\eta\in(0,\eta_{0}]
, which means the functional
\mathscr{E}_{\eta,\eta_{1}}(G,E,B)
is nonnegative for all
\eta\in(0,\eta_{0}]
. Furthermore, we have

(3.192)

\displaystyle\|G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|^{2}_{H^{s}}\leq 2\mathscr{E}_{\eta}(G,E,B)\,,

and

(3.193)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}B\|^{2}_{H^{s-2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq C(1+\tfrac{1}{\eta})\mathscr{D}_{\eta}(G,E,B)

for all
\eta\in(0,\eta_{0}]
.

Consequently, we have derived the following proposition from the inequality (
3.189
):

Proposition 3.6
.

Assume that
(G,E,B)
is the solution to the perturbed VMB system (
1.9
) constructed in Proposition
3.1
. Then there are constants
\eta_{0},\eta_{1}\in(0,1]
and
C>0
, independent of
\varepsilon>0
, such that

(3.194)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathscr{E}_{\eta}(G,E,B)+\varepsilon\eta\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathscr{A}_{s}(G,E,B)(t)+\mathscr{D}_{\eta}(G,E,B)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(1+\tfrac{1}{\eta})\big[\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\eta}(G,E,B)+\mathscr{E}^{2}_{\eta}(G,E,B)\big]\mathscr{D}_{\eta}(G,E,B)

\displaystyle+C\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\eta}(G,E,B)\mathscr{D}_{w}(G)

holds for all
0<\eta\leq\eta_{0}
and
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
.

Remark 3.3
.

The parameter
\eta\in(0,\eta_{0}]
given in Proposition
3.6
is small to be determined such that the unsigned functional
\varepsilon\eta\mathscr{A}_{s}(G,E,B)
defined in (
3.190
) will be dominated by
\mathscr{E}_{\eta}(G,E,B)
for all
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
.

One notices that the energy inequality (
3.194
) is not closed, because so far the quantity
\mathscr{D}_{w}(G)
is uncontrolled.

4.
Energy estimates for the
(x,v)
-mixed derivatives and global solutions

In this section, based on the bound (
3.194
) in Proposition
3.6
, we will derive the energy estimates on the
(x,v)
-mixed derivatives of the kinetic part
\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
to control the the energy functional
\mathscr{D}_{\eta}(G)
defined in (
3.190
). Then we can obtain a closed energy inequality of the perturbed VMB system (
3.2
), which is uniform in
\varepsilon\in(0,1]
. One notices that for the hydrodynamic part
\mathbb{P}G
,

(4.1)

\displaystyle\|p(v)\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\leq C\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

holds for any polynomial
p(v)
, which has been estimated in Proposition
3.6
. Furthermore, when we compute the
L^{2}_{x,v}
-norm of
\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
for
|m|+|\alpha|\leq s
with
\alpha\neq 0
in the later, there is an uncontrolled term
\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|m^{\prime}|+|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq s\\
\alpha^{\prime}<\alpha\end{subarray}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}
in the right-hand side of (
4.58
). However, we observe that the orders of
v
-derivatives in this term is strictly less that
|\alpha|
, so that we can employ an induction over
|\alpha|
, which ranges from
0
to
s
. To be more precise, one can first inductively derive the following lemma.

Lemma 4.1
.

Assume that
(G,E,B)
is the solution to the perturbed VMB system (
1.9
) constructed in Proposition
3.1
. Let
s\geq 3
be any fixed integer. For any given
0\leq k\leq s
,
|\alpha|\leq k
, there are positive constants
C_{|\alpha|}
,
C_{k}^{*}
,
\delta_{k}
,
\delta^{*}_{k}
,
\varrho_{k}
and
\varrho^{*}_{k}
, independent of
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
and
0<\eta\leq\eta_{0}
, such that

(4.2)

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\bigg\{\varrho_{k}\mathscr{E}_{\eta}(G,E,B)+\varrho^{*}_{k}\varepsilon\eta\mathscr{A}_{s}(G,E,B)+\sum_{|m|+|\alpha|\leq s\,,\,|\alpha|\leq k}\tfrac{C_{|\alpha|}\eta}{1+\eta}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{\delta_{k}\eta}{1+\eta}\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{|m|+|\alpha|\leq s\,,\,|\alpha|\leq k}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}+\delta^{*}_{k}\mathscr{D}_{\eta}(G,E,B)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}^{*}\Big\{\mathscr{E}_{\eta}^{\frac{1}{2}}(G,E,B)\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{\widetilde{H}^{s}_{x,v}(\nu)}+(1+\tfrac{1}{\eta})\big[\mathscr{E}_{\eta}^{\frac{1}{2}}(G,E,B)+\mathscr{E}_{\eta}^{2}(G,E,B)\big]

\displaystyle+(1+\mathscr{E}_{\eta}(G,E,B))\big(\mathscr{E}_{\eta}(G,E,B)+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{\widetilde{H}^{s}_{x,v}(\nu)}\big)\Big\}\mathscr{D}_{\eta}(G,E,B)

holds for all
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
and
0<\eta\leq\eta_{0}
, where the constant
\eta_{0}>0
is mentioned in Proposition
3.6
. Here the functionals
\mathscr{E}_{\eta}(G,E,B)
,
\mathscr{A}_{s}(G,E,B)
and
\mathscr{D}_{\eta}(G,E,B)
are defined in (
3.190
).

Proof of Lemma
4.1
.

We now rewrite the first
G
-equation of (
3.2
) as

(4.3)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\big[v\cdot\nabla_{x}+\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\big]\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\mathscr{L}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}(E\cdot v)\sqrt{M}\mathsf{q}_{1}+\tfrac{1}{2}\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v)G+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\Gamma(G,G)

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}\mathbb{P}G-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\big[v\cdot\nabla_{x}+\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\big]\mathbb{P}G\,.

For all
|m|+|\alpha|\leq s
and
\alpha\neq 0
, we take the derivative operator
\partial^{m}_{\alpha}
in the equation (
4.3
) and then we obtain

(4.4)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\big[v\cdot\nabla_{x}+\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\big]\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathscr{L}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\big[(E\cdot v)\sqrt{M}\mathsf{q}_{1}\big]+\tfrac{1}{2}\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\big[\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v)G\big]+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\Gamma(G,G)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}C_{\alpha}^{\alpha^{\prime}}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}C_{\alpha}^{\alpha^{\prime}}\mathsf{q}(\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}<m}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq 1}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}C_{\alpha}^{\alpha^{\prime}}\mathsf{q}(\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}v\times\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}G-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\big[v\cdot\nabla_{x}+\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\big]\mathbb{P}G\,.

We take the inner product of (
4.4
) over
\mathbb{T}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
with
\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
. More precisely, we obtain

(4.5)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\underset{M_{1}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathscr{L}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underset{M_{2}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\big[(E\cdot v)\sqrt{M}\mathsf{q}_{1}\big],\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}+\underset{M_{3}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{2}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\big[\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v)G\big],\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle+\underset{M_{4}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\Gamma(G,G),\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}\ \underset{M_{5}}{\underbrace{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}C_{\alpha}^{\alpha^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{M_{6}}{\underbrace{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}C_{\alpha}^{\alpha^{\prime}}\left\langle\mathsf{q}(\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{m}_{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{M_{7}}{\underbrace{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}<m}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq 1}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}C_{\alpha}^{\alpha^{\prime}}\left\langle\mathsf{q}(\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}v\times\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{M_{8}}{\underbrace{-\left\langle\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}G,\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}\ \underset{M_{9}}{\underbrace{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\big[v\cdot\nabla_{x}+\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\big]\mathbb{P}G,\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}\,.

Recalling the decomposition of
\mathscr{L}
in Lemma
2.3
(1), we have

(4.6)

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathscr{L}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}=2\left\langle\partial^{m}_{\alpha}(\nu(v)\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G),\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}-\left\langle\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathscr{K}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\,.

From Lemma
2.2
(2), we derive

(4.7)

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial^{m}_{\alpha}(\nu(v)\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G),\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\geq C_{5}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}-C_{6}\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}<\alpha}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\,.

Moreover, Lemma
2.3
(2) tells us that for any
\delta>0
, there is a
C(\delta)>0
such that

(4.8)

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathscr{K}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\leq\delta\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}+C(\delta)\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\,.

Thus taking
\delta=C_{5}>0
,
\lambda_{0}=C_{5}>0
and
\lambda_{1}=C(C_{5})+2C_{6}>0
implies that the quantity
M_{1}
has the lower bound

(4.9)

\displaystyle M_{1}=\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathscr{L}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\geq\tfrac{\lambda_{0}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}-\tfrac{\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}<\alpha}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\,.

Since
|m|+|\alpha|\leq s
and
\alpha\neq 0
,
0\leq|m|\leq s-1
. Then the term
M_{2}
can be estimated as

(4.10)

\displaystyle M_{2}=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}E\cdot\partial^{\alpha}_{v}(v\sqrt{M}\mathsf{q}_{1}),\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}_{\alpha}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{v}(v\sqrt{M}\mathsf{q}_{1})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\leq\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{\gamma}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}+C_{\gamma}\|E\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\

for small
\gamma>0
to be determined, where the Hölder inequality, Young’s inequality and Part (1) of Lemma
2.2
are utilized here.

Next we estimate the term
M_{3}
. It can be directly calculated by using the decomposition
G=\mathbb{P}G+\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G
that

(4.11)

\displaystyle M_{3}=

\displaystyle\underset{M_{31}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq 1}C_{\alpha}^{\alpha^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}E\cdot(\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}v\otimes\mathsf{q}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}\mathbb{P}G),\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underset{M_{32}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{m^{\prime}<m}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq 1}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}C_{\alpha}^{\alpha^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\cdot(\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}v\otimes\mathsf{q}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbb{P}G),\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underset{M_{33}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq 1}C_{\alpha}^{\alpha^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}E\cdot(\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}v\otimes\mathsf{q}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G),\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underset{M_{34}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{m^{\prime}<m}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq 1}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}C_{\alpha}^{\alpha^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\cdot(\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}v\otimes\mathsf{q}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G),\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}\,.

For the term
M_{31}
, we drive from the Hölder inequality, the Sobolev embedding
H^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{\infty}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
, the inequality (
4.1
) and the part (1) of Lemma

that

(4.12)

\displaystyle M_{31}\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq 1}\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}v\otimes\mathsf{q}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\leq C\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\,.

Here we require the integer
s\geq 2
. For the
M_{32}
, we additionally derive from the Sobolev embedding
H^{1}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{4}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
that

(4.13)

\displaystyle M_{32}\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{m^{\prime}<m}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq 1}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}v\otimes\mathsf{q}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{4}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{m^{\prime}<m}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq 1}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\,.

In the term
M_{33}
, if
m=0
and
1\leq|\alpha|\leq s
, which satisfy
|m|+|\alpha|\leq s
and
\alpha\neq 0
, one easily derive from the Hölder inequality, the part (1) of Lemma
2.1
and the Sobolev embedding
H^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{\infty}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
and
H^{1}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{4}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
that

(4.14)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq 1}C_{\alpha}^{\alpha^{\prime}}\left\langle E\cdot(\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}v\otimes\mathsf{q}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G),\partial^{\alpha}_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\|E\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\left\|\tfrac{\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}v}{\nu(v)}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{4}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle+\|E\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\tfrac{v}{\nu(v)}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\|E\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}+C\|E\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\,.

Here
s\geq 2
is required. In the term
M_{33}
, if
m,\alpha\neq 0
and
|m|+|\alpha|\leq s
, we have
1\leq|m|,|\alpha|\leq s-1
. Then we estimate that

(4.15)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq 1}C_{\alpha}^{\alpha^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m}_{x}E\cdot(\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}v\otimes\mathsf{q}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G),\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq 1}\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\big\|\tfrac{\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}v}{\nu(v)}\big\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{4}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq 1}\|\partial^{m}_{x}E\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\,,

where we make use of the Sobolev embedding
H^{1}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{4}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
. We thereby obtain the bound of
M_{33}

(4.16)

\displaystyle M_{33}\leq

\displaystyle C\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

holds for all
|m|+|\alpha|\leq s
and
\alpha\neq 0
. In the term

, since

and

. We then deduce that

(4.17)

\displaystyle M_{34}\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{m^{\prime}<m}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq 1}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big\|\tfrac{\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{v}v}{\nu(v)}\big\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{m^{\prime}<m}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq 1}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m,\,\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\,,

where the Sobolev embedding
H^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{\infty}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
is used. We then derive the bound of
M_{3}
from plugging the inequalities (

), (

), (

) and (

) into the equality (

) that

(4.18)

\displaystyle M_{3}\leq

\displaystyle C\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m,\,\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle C\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}+C\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{\widetilde{H}^{s}_{x,v}(\nu)}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

for all
|m|+|\alpha|\leq s
and
\alpha\neq 0
.

Next we estimate the term
M_{4}
in (
4.5
). Via Lemma
2.4
and the decomposition

, we easily estimate that

(4.19)

\displaystyle M_{4}\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C_{\Gamma}}{\varepsilon}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x,v}}\|G\|_{H^{s}_{x,v}(\nu)}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\big(\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x,v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x,v}}\big)\big(\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x,v}(\nu)}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x,v}(\nu)}\big)\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\,.

Furthermore, by the Poincaré inequality, the relation (
4.1
) and Proposition

, we have

(4.20)

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x,v}}+\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x,v}(\nu)}\leq C\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+C\big(|(\rho^{+})_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|+|(\rho^{-})_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|+|(u)_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|+|(\theta)_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\big(\|\rho^{+}-(\rho^{+})_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\rho^{-}-(\rho^{-})_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|u-(u)_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\theta-(\theta)_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)

\displaystyle C\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+C(\|E\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{1}_{x}})(\|E\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{L^{2}_{x}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+C(\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})(\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{H^{s-2}_{x}})\,.

Here
s\geq 2
is required. We thereby obtain

(4.21)

\displaystyle M_{4}\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\big(\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x,v}}\big)\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x,v}(\nu)}\big)\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}(\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\big(\|\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x,v}}\big)

\displaystyle\times(\|E\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{H^{s-2}_{x}})\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\,.

Next we consider the quantity

in (
4.5

, one easily yields that

(4.22)

\displaystyle M_{5}\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{\gamma}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}+C_{\gamma}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|m^{\prime}|+|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq s\\

for small

to be determined.

The term

in (

(4.23)

\displaystyle M_{6}\leq

Next we estimate the term

in (

(4.24)

\displaystyle M_{7}=

\displaystyle\underset{M_{72}}{\underbrace{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}<m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\mathsf{q}(v\times\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}\,.

,

,

and

in the term

Then the term

can be estimated by

(4.25)

\displaystyle M_{71}\leq

2.2
. In the term

(1) are utilized. Therefore, the bounds (

) reduce to

(4.29)

\displaystyle M_{72}\leq\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{\widetilde{H}^{s}_{x,v}(\nu)}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

for all
|m|+|\alpha|\leq s
and
\alpha\neq 0
. As a consequence, substituting the bounds (
4.25
) and (
4.29
) into the equality (
4.24
) tells us

(4.30)

\displaystyle M_{7}\leq\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{\widetilde{H}^{s}_{x,v}(\nu)}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\,.

Next we estimate the term
M_{8}
in the relation (
4.5
). Recalling the expression of
\mathbb{P}G
in (
1.16
), i.e.,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}G=\rho^{+}\phi_{1}(v)+\rho^{-}\phi_{2}(v)+\sum_{i=1}^{3}u_{i}\phi_{i+2}(v)+\theta\phi_{6}(v)\,,

we estimate the term
M_{8}
as

(4.31)

\displaystyle M_{8}=

\displaystyle-\left\langle\partial_{t}(\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\phi_{1}+\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\phi_{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{3}\partial^{m}_{x}u_{i}\phi_{i+2}+\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\phi_{6}),\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\,,

where we make use of the fact
\phi_{i}(v)\in L^{2}_{v}
for
1\leq i\leq 6
. Recall that the hydrodynamic coefficients
\rho^{\pm}
,
u
and
\theta
obey the relations (
3.34
), namely,

(4.32)

\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\partial_{t}\rho^{+}=\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\big[-\mathrm{div}_{x}u+\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\phi_{1}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big]\,,\\[5.69054pt]
\partial_{t}\rho^{-}=\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\big[-\mathrm{div}_{x}u+\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\phi_{2}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big]\,,\\[5.69054pt]
\partial_{t}u_{i}=\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\big[-\partial_{i}(\tfrac{\rho^{+}+\rho^{-}}{2}+\theta)+\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\phi_{i+2}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big]\ \ \textrm{for}\ \ 1\leq i\leq 3\,,\\[5.69054pt]
\partial_{t}\theta=\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\big[-\tfrac{2}{3}\mathrm{div}_{x}u+\tfrac{1}{3}\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\phi_{6}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big]\,.\end{array}\right.

For the quantity
\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}
, we derive from the first equation of (
4.32
) and the expression of
\mathbb{P}G
in (
1.16
) that for all
|m|\leq s-1

(4.33)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{div}_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\partial^{m}_{x}\left\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)+\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\phi_{1}(v)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\underset{M_{81}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\|\partial^{m}_{x}\langle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G),\phi_{1}(v)\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}}}+\underset{M_{82}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\|\partial^{m}_{x}\langle\Psi(\mathbb{P}G),\phi_{1}(v)\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}}}\,.

Recalling that
\phi_{1}(v)\in\textrm{Ker}(\mathscr{L})
,
(\partial_{t}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathscr{L})\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\in\textrm{Ker}^{\perp}(\mathscr{L})
and
\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)
defined in (
3.30
), i.e.,

\displaystyle\Theta(\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G)=-\left(\varepsilon\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathscr{L}+\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}-\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v)\right)\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\,,

one easily has

(4.34)

\displaystyle M_{81}=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\|\partial^{m}_{x}\langle(v\cdot\nabla_{x}+\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}-\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v))\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\phi_{1}(v)\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\underset{M_{811}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\|\langle v\cdot\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\phi_{1}(v)\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}}}+\underset{M_{812}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\|\partial^{m}_{x}\langle\varepsilon\mathsf{q}E\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\phi_{1}(v)\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underset{M_{813}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\|\partial^{m}_{x}\langle\mathsf{q}(v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\phi_{1}(v)\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}}}+\underset{M_{814}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\|\partial^{m}_{x}\langle\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v)\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\phi_{1}(v)\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

for
|m|\leq s-1
. Since
\phi_{1}(v)
contains the exponential decay factor
\exp\big(-\tfrac{|v|^{2}}{4}\big)
, we know that
p(v)\phi_{1}(v)\in L^{2}_{v}
for any polynomial
p(v)
. As a result, we derive from the Hölder inequality and Lemma
2.2
(1) that the term
M_{811}
is bounded by

(4.35)

\displaystyle M_{811}\leq\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|v\otimes\phi_{1}(v)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\leq\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\leq\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\,.

In the term
M_{812}
, since
|m|\leq s-1
, the relation
|m-m^{\prime}|+1=|m|-|m^{\prime}|+1\leq|m|\leq s-1
holds if
m^{\prime}\neq 0
. Then we compute that

(4.36)

\displaystyle M_{812}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\big\|\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\mathsf{q}\phi_{1}(v)\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|E\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\nabla_{v}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\mathsf{q}\phi_{1}(v)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\|\nabla_{v}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{4}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\mathsf{q}\phi_{1}(v)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|E\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla_{v}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+C\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\|\nabla_{v}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\nabla_{v}\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{\widetilde{H}^{s}_{x,v}(\nu)}\,,

where we make use of the Hölder inequality, the Sobolev embedding
H^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{\infty}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
,
H^{1}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{4}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
and the part (1) of Lemma
2.2
. Here
s\geq 2
is required. In the term
M_{813}
, since
|m^{\prime}|,|m-m^{\prime}|\leq|m|\leq s-1
, we derive from the integration by parts over
v\in\mathbb{T}^{3}
, the Sobolev embedding
H^{1}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{4}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
, the Hölder inequality and the part (1) of Lemma
2.2
that

(4.37)

\displaystyle M_{813}=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\|-\partial^{m}_{x}\langle\mathsf{q}(v\times B)\otimes\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\nabla_{v}\phi_{1}(v)\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\big\|\langle\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}B\otimes\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\mathsf{q}(v\times\nabla_{v})\phi_{1}(v)\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}B\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{4}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\mathsf{q}(v\times\nabla_{v})\phi_{1}(v)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}B\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\leq\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\,.

The term
M_{814}
in (
4.34
) can be estimated by

(4.38)

\displaystyle M_{814}\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\big\|\langle\mathsf{q}(\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\cdot v)\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G,\phi_{1}(v)\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{4}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|v\otimes\phi_{1}(v)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}E\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\leq C\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\,,

where the Hölder inequality, the Sobolev embedding
H^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{\infty}_{x}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
and Lemma
2.2
(1) are utilized. Plugging the bounds (
4.35
), (
4.36
), (
4.37
) and (
4.38
) into (

) reduces to

(4.39)

\displaystyle M_{81}\leq\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\big(\varepsilon\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\big)\big(\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{\widetilde{H}^{s}_{x,v}(\nu)}\big)\,.

Next we estimate the term
M_{82}
in (
4.33
). Recalling the expression of
\Psi(\mathbb{P}G)
defined in (
3.31
), i.e.,

\displaystyle\Psi(\mathbb{P}G)=-\mathsf{q}(\varepsilon E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}G+\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\mathsf{q}(E\cdot v)\mathbb{P}G\,,

one can derive bound of the
M_{82}
that

(4.40)

\displaystyle M_{82}\leq

\displaystyle\underset{M_{821}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\|\partial^{m}_{x}\langle\varepsilon E\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}G,\mathsf{q}\phi_{1}(v)\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}}}+\underset{M_{822}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\|\partial^{m}_{x}\langle(v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}G,\mathsf{q}\phi_{1}(v)\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}}}

\displaystyle+\underset{M_{823}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\|\partial^{m}_{x}\langle\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon(E\cdot v)\mathbb{P}G,\mathsf{q}\phi_{1}(v)\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}}}\,.

For the term
M_{821}
in (
4.40
), we have

(4.41)

\displaystyle M_{821}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\big\|\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathsf{q}\phi_{1}(v),\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle C\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\nabla_{v}\mathsf{q}\phi_{1}(v)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle C\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\leq C\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\,,

. For the terms
M_{822}
and

in (

), we similarly estimate that

(4.42)

\displaystyle M_{822}\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\big\|\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}B\otimes\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G,v\times\nabla_{v}(\mathsf{q}\phi_{1}(v))\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}B\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|v\times\nabla_{v}(\mathsf{q}\phi_{1}(v))\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}B\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

and

(4.43)

\displaystyle M_{823}\leq

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}E\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|v\otimes\mathsf{q}\phi_{1}(v)\|_{L^{6}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}E\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\leq C\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\,.

Substituting the inequality (
4.41
), (
4.42
) and (
4.43
) into the relation (

), we obtain

(4.44)

\displaystyle M_{82}\leq\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}(\varepsilon\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\,.

We thereby deduce from plugging the bounds (
4.39
) and (
4.44
) into the relation (

) that

(4.45)

\displaystyle\|

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{+}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}(\varepsilon\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{\widetilde{H}^{s}_{x,v}(\nu)}\big)\,.

Furthermore, via the analogous argument in estimating the norm

in (
4.45
), one can easily yield that

(4.46)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}\rho^{-}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}\partial^{m}_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}(\varepsilon\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{\widetilde{H}^{s}_{x,v}(\nu)}\big)\,.

We then plug the bounds (

) and (

) into the relation (

(4.47)

\displaystyle M_{8}\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}(\varepsilon\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\times\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{\widetilde{H}^{s}_{x,v}(\nu)}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\eta}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\big)+\tfrac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}(\varepsilon\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|B\|_{H^{s}_{x}})\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\times\big(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{H^{s}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}+\|\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{\widetilde{H}^{s}_{x,v}(\nu)}\big)

for small
\eta>0
to be determined, where the Young’s inequality and Lemma
2.2
are utilized.

Next we estimate the term
M_{9}
in (
4.5
). We first decompose it as three parts and then estimate them term by term. More precisely,

(4.48)

\displaystyle M_{9}=

\displaystyle\underset{M_{91}}{\underbrace{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle v\cdot\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}G,\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}\ \underset{M_{92}}{\underbrace{-\left\langle\mathsf{q}\partial^{m}_{\alpha}(E\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}G),\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}

\displaystyle\underset{M_{93}}{\underbrace{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\mathsf{q}\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\big((v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}G\big),\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}}\,.

Since

, it is easily derived from the Hölder inequality, the bound (

), the Young’s inequality and Lemma

\displaystyle M_{91}\leq

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\gamma}\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\tfrac{\gamma}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

for small
\gamma>0
to be determined. For the term
M_{92}
in (

), we employ the Hölder inequality, the Sobolev embedding

, the Sobolev interpolation inequality

, the bound (

) and the part (1) of Lemma

to estimate

(4.50)

\displaystyle M_{92}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\mathsf{q}(\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}G),\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|\nabla_{v}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{x}E\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|E\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}\mathbb{P}G\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\,.

Similarly in (
4.50
), one can calculate that

(4.51)

\displaystyle M_{93}=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\mathsf{q}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}B\cdot\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{\alpha}(v\times\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}G),\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}B\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{\alpha}(v\times\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}G)\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}_{x}B\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}\partial^{m^{\prime}}_{\alpha}(v\times\nabla_{v}\mathbb{P}G)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\partial^{m}_{\alpha}\mathbb{P}^{\perp}G\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq
